At that moment, he was almost spitting out his intestines, so he even hit his head on the railing and mumbled like he was losing consciousness.

“Uh... my head. I feel dizzy... sahyung... if this continues, I might die before I even fight.”

“Euk, I don’t think it was this bad before.”

“Tell him to slow down the speed!”

“Soso. Do you not have any medicine for motion sickness?”

“... do such things exist?”

“Ugh.”

Didn’t one get used to this by riding the ship?

Unfortunately, the people gathered on the ship were from Sichuan, where there was no river even if one looked for it, and from Shaanxi, where only mountains existed.

Since it was a warrior’s place, they could overcome a little bit of seasickness. Still, there was nothing they could do on a ship that was shaking so violently that even sailors were finding it difficult.

“Island! Where is the damned island!”

“Please... please let me just fight.”

“I feel like I can do anything as long as I put my feet on the ground! Please!”

By nature, people only knew something was precious to them when they lost it. Mount Hua’s disciples were also endlessly missing the existence of land below their feet, which they always took for granted.

“Eukk...”

At that time, Im So-Byeong, who had gone from a pure white face to pale blue, stumbled to Chung Myung.

“You...”

“Uh?”

Jo Seung, who was holding his breath for fear of life, was startled by Im So-Byeong’s gesture and jumped up from his place. Im So-Byeong asked in a dying voice.

“Island... where is the island? Is it far?”

“The exact location... is... I don’t know for sure....”

“You don’t know?”

“Ah, no. I know. But....”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes turned red at the response.

“Should I skin you alive and sprinkle salt on the flesh? How well can you dance in that state?”

“S-spare me, please!”

Jo Seung, who remembered once again that this person was the Green Forest King, became thoughtful of his words and shook his head.

“No, there is something you can see when we get... there, over there! That place! Ugh! I can see it there!”

“Ugh. Where?”

“There!”

Jo Seung, who found a way to survive, pointed ahead.

Sure enough, there was something small floating in the middle of the river.

“... But, is that an island?”

“It looks small now because it is far, but it surely is not small. It is actually bigger than where our base was.”

“What did you say?”

“Actually, it is better too...”

“No, before that? What? Is it far?”

“...”

“Eukkkk! Eukkkkkk!”

“Ahh! Don’t throw up here! That’s disgusting!”

Chung Myung hurriedly grabbed Im So-Byeong by the back of his neck and threw him to the railing. Then he shook his hands, looking at the island in the distance.

“Hmm.”

It was hard to see the island clearly because of the thick reeds, but it certainly seemed like it would be big enough for a pirate’s base.

“Interesting. In the middle of the river.”

Chung Myung mumbled and touched his chin but suddenly stopped.

“Hmm?”

A smile slowly formed on his lips.

“Yah, water ghost.”

“... Yes!”

“It seems like they are coming to meet us from there?”

“Huh?”

Jo Seung lifted his head and looked toward the island.

“What?”

As expected.

Small dot-like things began to appear around the island in the distance. If it was a dot floating on the water, it wasn’t hard to guess what it could be.

As the dots continued to get closer, they began to take the shape of ships.

“I-it is the pirates! Get ready for battle!”

“B-battle?”

“In this situation?”

The eyes of the bandits shook.

Their stomachs were a mess... sure, they could fight while puking, but it would be unsightly. And what about the headaches and dizziness? It was hard to stand up properly.

“Ugh. I am dying.”

Only then did the bandits realize how advantageous the previous battle was. Fighting the pirates on land was no different from fighting sharks that had been pulled ashore.

Even if they were cats, they didn’t have to get too close to a tiger; they could easily eat the struggling shark.

But if the location was a river, the entire situation changed. Even if it was a tiger and not a cat, they would end up as a meal for the shark in the water.

‘This is why we cannot mess with the pirates in water.’

“Ugh. I don’t get it. It seems like a natural fortress.”

Before he knew it, Im So-Byeong came back from puking and spoke with a pale face.

“Why were the pirates neglecting such a place?”

“Because there is no place to sink.”

“Why?”

“First, isn’t that place too noticeable?”

“... right.”

“Furthermore, the water around there has wild waves because it is an island. The flow speed is twice as fast, and the water channels all seem mixed, creating a vortex in an instant. No matter how skillful a captain is, a ship will capsize if they aren’t careful. So how can one create a pirate base in such a place?”

“But those people did it, right?”

“... those people are...”

Jo Seung scratched his head with a grim look.

“Anyway, it’s because it’s those bastards, right?”

Chung Myung’s eyes became bright as he watched them.

“Let’s make them all into fish food and then think about it! Everyone to the battle....”

Kwaaang!

“Uh?”

But at that moment, with the sound of lightning falling, the ship next to them was destroyed.

“F-fire?”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened as he glanced at the ships approaching from the other side. He could clearly see the black and white smoke mixing and spreading out.

“They’re using cannons too? Are those guys insane?”

Gunpowder was a weapon prohibited by the government. In other words, from the moment they used gunpowder, they would be tracked down. Salt and gunpowder were the most targeted items by the officials, so one could not carelessly touch them, right?

The pirates had mixed a small amount of gunpowder with this harpoon-like device and fired it. Still, no matter how they looked, the weapons on that ship seemed like a full-on artillery unit.

“That... it looks like the White Thunder Cannon?”

Chung Myung turned his head at Im So-Byeong’s words.

“White Thunder Cannon? What is that?”

“That... well, it is a piece of artillery made by a place called the White Thunder Clan, which was really famous in the past.”

“... if it is the White Thunder Clan, then is it a martial clan?”

“Yes. It is a clan that used gunpowder a lot.”

“Are they made of insane people? There are bastards like that, and the officials just watched?”

“Would they have watched? That is the reason the clan got ruined.”

“...”

“But the artillery and bombs they manufactured are still being secretly traded. It looks like the pirates got to them...”

Kwaaang!

The head of another ship was completely blown off.

“AHHH! We are sinking!”

“Ugh! Pueah! Help me! I cannot swim!”

“B-be careful of the water! Those bastards will come after you! Grab a plank and climb up! Or get on the ship next to you, you bastards!”

In an instant, everything turned to chaos.

“Come on, harpoon! We have a harpoon too, right?”

“... ehh. Icouldn’t’t possibly reach that far.”

Jo Seung, who was watching, waved his hand.

“Uh? They are slowing down?”

“Uh?”

“It seems like they plan to keep their distance and destroy us.”

“Those cowardly bastards! If they are warriors, they should fight fairly!”

“... they are pirates!”

Things were fair and square only when needed, but even that felt meaningless in the face of pirates.

Kwaang!

“Ackkk!”

The moment another ship got destroyed, Chung Myung rolled his eyes.

“These bastards are crossing the damn line?!”

“Oh?”

Im So-Byeong looked back at Chung Myung with a touched expression.

The disciples of Mount Hua were on this ship. The story went that only the ships with bandits got cracked. But seeing him get angry, doesn’t that mean Chung Myung considers the Green Forest bandits his true colleagues?

“We are alright....”

“How big are all their fucking ships!”

“....”

Ahh... so that was added to their property list?

How nice. He was embarrassed for him as a bandit.

“Okay! They are shooting artillery, right?”

At that moment, Chung Myung jumped up to the railing.

And!

“I will tell them that even a stomach has a head of its own! Okayyyyy!”

Kwang!

He kicked the railing, making it crack, and began to fly like a cannon in the air.

Chapter 772: If You Fall Behind, You All Die! (2)

Paaat!

Shells flew through the air, and a human cannonball flew in from the other side.

Chung Myung, who closed a few meters in one leap, soared above the water again.

“What? What is that thing?”

“How can a human fly?”

The pirates who were shooting opened their mouths in confusion. But there seemed to be some who remained calm among them.

“What are you doing! Shoot! Shoot him down!”

“Shoot!”

The guns targeting the ship simultaneously turned their cannons toward Chung Myung.

“Shoot!”

Kwaang!

The White Thunder Cannon began to fire again.

Black shells rained down on Chung Myung, who was now running on water.

“Uh?”

Chung Myung looked up at the scene and twisted his lips into a smile.

Swish!

His body moved on the water, shifting irregularly here and there like falling flower petals.

Puak! Puak!

The cannonballs fell on the water he passed through, and more than a dozen huge water columns rose up. The sight of water rising like pillars in an instant on the Yangtze River was a spectacle.

“Shoot! Continue shooting!”

Even though they had never seen Chung Myung before, it wasn’t difficult to guess what would happen if this guy running on water reached them safely. So, the pirates desperately fired the cannons.

“What! Why did those bastards pack so much gunpowder? Do all the officials sleep?”

Chung Myung didn’t particularly agree with the government’s position. Since he was the one dealing with the cannons now, he was upset with them.

But he had no time to curse the officials at the moment. The cannons continued to fly.

Chung Myung’s face contorted as he sped up and rushed ahead.

“Ughh.”

But even for him, running constantly on the water was not an easy task.

And at that moment.

“Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon’s voice rang loudly behind him. Chung Myung rose from the surface without a second thought.

Tak! Tak!

Eventually, he stepped onto the wooden planks, which flew in from behind and moved ahead. The eyes of Chung Myung shone as the shells continued to come.

“Ahhh!”

His body, spinning in the air, shot forward.

Tatak!

Kicking the cannon balls away, he moved at the speed of a flash.

“W-what is that?”

“Fuck!”

The attacking pirates were all shocked.

Running on the water was amazing in itself, but jumping between shells was even more shocking.

They couldn’t believe it when they saw it with their own eyes.

“Ughhh!”

Chung Myung flew right in front of the leading ship and pulled out his sword in the air.

Soon, his sword cut through the air with a terrifying roar, and a stream of red sword qi spewed out.

Paaang!

Half-moon-shaped red sword Qi flew to the bottom of the ship, which was in the water.

Slash!

The deck, made of hardwood, was cut down like paper.

“T-that...”

Kwaaang!

As soon as the wood turned into pieces, the cold water of the Yangtze River began to rush into the ship.

“I-it is sinking!”

“Damn it, jump in!”

If they jumped in with their bare bodies, they might kill themselves, but if they sank along with the ship, their lives couldn’t be guaranteed.

The pirates threw themselves into the Yangtze River without even looking back.

“Tsk!”

Chung Myung got off the bow of the lead ship and, without delay, kicked the ship and jumped towards the next one.

“Shoot at me again, you bastards!”

Paat!

His long sword qi, which erupted from the swing, dug into the deck. The ship was completely pierced.

“Attack! Do not let him step onto another ship!”

As soon as Chung Myung landed on the ship that had been cut, the pirates rushed at him, screaming.

But even if the opponent were rabid, Chung Myung was worse.

Kwang!

Chung Myung kicked the person running in front without delay and threw him into the cabin, mumbling with blazing eyes.

“These bastards!”

Of course, Chung Myung had handled so many pirates so far.

When it came to getting him annoyed, there was none close to the Southern Edge Sect, and when it came to annoying someone, it had to be Wudang. And the ones who could provoke anger from within him would be the Ten Thousand People Clan and the Demonic Sect? Don’t even mention those last bastards!

But these bastards were giving him a different feeling from those.

“How dare these fucking Evil Faction people glare at me? I will gouge your eyes out! You bastards!”

Kwang!

A person hit on the chin by the handle of the sword bounced back like a ball and fell into the river.

“The Great Mount Hua’s!”

Kwang!

The water pierced through the thick wooden deck in the shape of a human being and disappeared into the invisible depths below.

“Plum Blossom Sword Saint!!!”

It should be alright since no one could hear him now.

Uh? There were people here too?

No, no. These people were trash, so they were just like bamboo growing in a lush forest.

Chung Myung, who suddenly let out the truth for the first time, swept across the deck like a raging storm.

“H-he is a monster!”

“R-run away! Run! Now!”

Regardless of whether they were pirates or not, it was natural that one would want to live. The pirates who lost their will to fight jumped over the railing and threw themselves into the water, not looking back.

“Where do you think you are going, you bastards! Come here!”

Chung Myung grabbed the ankle of one pirate who was diving into the water and pulled him back.

“Eikk! S-spare me! I did nothing!”

“Did this bastard leave his brain at home? Does it make sense to say you did nothing?”

“Eikk!”

Chung Myung lifted the pirate up by the ankle and then swung his arm once, slamming the pirate onto the deck.

Kwaaak!

The deck collapsed, and the ship was almost done.

“Next!”

Chung Myung jumped to the next ship.

“This is water arts, you bastards!”

No.

“Woah...”

“So amazing...”

“Euk...”

“Ah, if you want to throw up, go over there and throw up!”

Mount Hua’s disciples shook their heads as they watched Chung Myung run around like an irritated wild boar.

Didn’t Chung Myung seem stronger than before?

If they thought about it, it was natural. That guy was originally strong, and he was the one who trained the most aggressively in Mount Hua. Baek Cheon, who had a reputation for training, had also been sick for days after trying to follow Chung Myung’s training plan. One can imagine how tough it was, right?

So, of course, he was bound to be stronger.

The problem was...

“Why... is his temper getting worse each day?”

“... who can know the answer to that?”

“Is Mount Hua that bad of a place to be...”

The word “rampage” felt apt for what Chung Myung was doing. The pirates looked pitiful now.

‘No, it cannot be.’

If they thought about it, this wasn’t an issue that could be solved so easily.

If they had kept their distance and the pirates continued to fire the White Thunder Cannon, they probably would have been drowning.

Those pirates were out of luck, too. Who would have dared to imagine that the guy on the ship would run across water, jump on wooden planks, step on shells, and jump into ships only to destroy them in an instant?

Those who thought normally with common sense would never be able to comprehend what just happened.

“I guess I will have to attack when they are out of their minds. Tell them to speed up!”

“Yes!”

Im So-Byeong pointed ahead with his fan.

“Move with your core! The moment we collide, we jump over and sweep them off!”

“Yes!”

The dignity of a strategist was evident in the sight of Im So-Byeong gently waving his fan...

“Woahh! Yah! Don’t speed up so much... kuak! Cough!”

... It couldn’t be seen.

“W-wait a moment. Getting closer now might not be a good chance.... Uh?”

At that time, Jo Seung, who was holding on to the railing, tilted his head.

“Why?”

“No, wait a minute...”

He looked closely at the scene ahead and spoke to Im So-Byeong.

“I think we can move.”

“Why are you saying one thing at a time? Speak up! This or that!”

“...normally, one shouldn’t approach pirates carelessly when they are in the water. Water pirates tend to feel more comfortable in the water than outside, so falling into the water doesn’t mean they are weak. If you get too close, they might make a hole under your ship.”

“Hmm?”

Im So-Byeong, who hadn’t thought of that, flinched for a moment.

“But then you are saying I can go? Aren’t you committing treason against your own?”

“Well, it isn’t like that. Look there. Aren’t they bad at controlling themselves?”

Im So-Byeong looked at the pirates who jumped off the sinking ship. There was something awkward about their splashing around.

“They haven’t learned water arts yet?”

“I think so.”

“I cannot believe that they haven’t learned it yet. Does that make sense?”

“T-that, in a way, it is expected...”

“What do you mean?”

Jo Seung gulped as he answered.

“Who is a born pirate? Usually, one either learns martial arts and then joins the pirates, or having no way to make a living, they join the pirates. And then they learn the martial arts.”

“Then, if the pirates were brought in recently, it isn’t strange for them to not swim right?”

“Yes! They probably aren’t pirates who moved here originally. There is a high chance they are newly brought in.”

Jo Seung’s face was quite desperate.

He didn’t know if the intention was to deny ties with those people or prevent the ties between Mount Hua and the pirates from worsening further.

“Hmm...”

Im So-Byeong nodded.

“Well, it isn’t something I care about, and anyway, it means there is no problem getting close, right? That is enough, then. Everyone, move on at full speed!”

The ship carrying the Mount Hua disciples and the bandit moved ahead swiftly.

The pirates’ ship, thrown into confusion by a single man, wasn’t aware of the ships approaching them, only realizing it later when they reached close to one another.

“A-avoid the ship!”

“It is crashing in!”

The large crash horn attached to the front of the ship hit the side of the pirate ship.

Kwaaang!

The ship hit on the side, tilted as if it was going to turn over, and got pushed away. However, the arrowhead-shaped horn, which pierced the side, held the hull tightly and didn’t let go.

“Walk over!”

The bandits threw the hook chains they had captured from the pirate base and tied them tightly together.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

The Mount Hua disciples drew their swords and rushed at once.

“Let’s go! Let us handle the pirates first!”

“Ohhh!”

The disciples of the famous sect, the Mount Hua sect, took the lead, followed by the bandits of Green Forest. If one looked at this combination, they would find it strange but strong at the same time.

Mount Hua’s disciples jumped between the ships and moved to the pirates’ ship, swinging their sharp swords to cut down the pirates in an instant.

“Kuaak!”

“T-these people!”

Baek Cheon struck down a pirate who was confused and counterattacked with a single blow to the chest. And he let out this roar filled with strength. No, he wanted to scream.

“Rush...!”

“Ohh, it is because of you that we almost died!”

“Do you know what happened because of you?”

“We almost died! You fucking pirates!”

“Die now!”

“....”

Guys? Their purpose in fighting seemed a bit strange now?

“Skin them and throw them in!”

“Yes!”

Baek Cheon looked at the Mount Hua disciples with blank looks, who were leading the bandits and rushing ahead.

Uh....

Hahah. Right, who cared even?

Just fight well; that should do.

“Throw them all down!”

Baek Cheon also joined in the strange sight without any regrets.

Chapter 773: If You Fall Behind, You All Die! (3)

“Dieeee!”

“Ahh! You pirate bastard!”

“I will kill you!”

Mount Hua’s disciples closed their eyes and charged at him.

“W-what?”

“What is wrong with these bastards!”

It was only natural to gain momentum against an opponent.

But there was a huge difference between gaining momentum and going full circle. Mount Hua’s disciples rushed at the pirates as if they had seen their mortal enemies.

“Ahhh! You bastards!”

“W-why are you being like this!”

It might be embarrassing for the pirates, but from the perspective of the Mount Hua disciples, it was completely natural.

How hard had they been training until now?

After meeting with the Wudang sect, they realized what they were lacking and trained to ensure that didn’t happen again. Didn’t they even devise new means to avoid being deficient in fights?

The fierceness wasn’t enough yet to show their true strength, but in any case, it was still an attack.

And they had no doubt that this battle would also require them to do their best, as they had learned to put in their utmost effort.

But...

“I will drag you by the stomach!”

“Get him caught in the net and flop around!”

“Drink poison!”

“Is this what people even do? You fucking bastards!”

Of course, that wasn’t what they did. But to the Mount Hua disciples, who hadn’t lived such a nuanced life, pirates were just pirates.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who rolled their eyes like Chung Myung, swung their swords. Looking at the sword’s bizarre path and the fierce force, one could believe that it wasn’t the Justice faction but the Evil Faction attacking and engaging in a battle for power.

“Ahhhh!”

Jo Gul’s sword spun as if it was about to crush a person to pieces.

“Y-You insane people!”

“I cannot stand them! Jump off!”

“Where do you people think you are going!”

The disciples of Mount Hua rushed at them, their eyes almost completely white with fury. At the same time, the pirates ran desperately and barely managed to throw themselves into the river.

“Are you trying to run?”

“Yah! Cross over, cross over! To the next ship!”

“Here I come!”

Seeing their wild eyes, the Green Forest bandits stayed silent.

‘Let us not get involved in this.’

‘They seem like those who live in the mountains... if they come over to our mountains, we won’t seem so special anymore.’

‘I need to tell everyone not to even think about fighting with the Mount Hua Sect.’

What could they say when the leader was running rampant all alone, and his subordinates were equally ferocious?

The guys here alone could devour a large mountain, too, if given the chance. Then, the place of Green Forest would also change...

“There! There!”

“There are a lot of pirate bastards over there!”

“Knock them all to death!”

“Um, Taoists... calm down a little...”

“What?”

“N-nothing...”

Who was the bandit and who was the Taoist here?

Fortunately or unfortunately, there was no one around serious enough to question the facts.

“Ughhh!”

Rumble!

Meanwhile, Chung Myung was sinking another ship in front of them.

At the front, he flew like a pirate king, destroying the ship, and at the back, the party with Baek Cheon, including the monk, led the bandits and crossed the ships, sweeping the pirates down.

“Kuaak!”

“D-damn it!”

A look of confusion was evident on the faces of the pirates who abandoned the ship and fled.

In the first place, if the pirates didn’t push back with their numbers, they would be no match for the Mount Hua disciples. The Five Swords were now stronger than most middle-level clans or sects.

Once they boarded the ship, there was no other way out for the pirates. On a ship with limited space, using their numerical advantage was tough, and it wasn’t easy to use different weapons.

Therefore, they had to fight based solely on their skills. Despite their shaky footing, the Mount Hua disciples were not going to struggle against the pirates.

Moreover,

“Ahhh! Follow them!”

“Mount Hua! Mount Hua! Mount Hua!”

“They aren’t Mount Hua; they are bandits! They are Mount Hua bandits!”

“Who cares about all that!”

The bandits, who liked to suppress the strong whenever they saw one, were swept away by the momentum of the Mount Hua disciples. They shouted loudly and drove the pirates away.

Im So-Byeong, who was watching the process while standing at the bow of the ship without crossing over, scratched the back of his head with his fan.

“This...”

His head slowly tilted to the side.

“Does this make sense?”

Of course, he had no complaints about this. On the contrary, it was clearly a situation that can be called joyful. But he couldn’t help but find it strange.

‘This isn’t a situation where the battle should happen like this.’

The other side took advantage of the land and fought the battle wisely. Even if Im So-Byeong personally commanded and fought with similar strength, he would end up taking a good amount of damage.

‘At least I would have to risk around half a ship being destroyed.’

It was inevitable with the artillery attacks that were happening. Even if the ancestor came back alive, he would only reduce a certain amount of damage. Still, it would be difficult to change the situation.

But that ridiculous human there jumped in alone and turned the battlefield upside down.

“Hahaha. It is turning.”

It was a moment when everything he had learned was denied, but Im So-Byeong was more happy than angry.

“Right. This is how one becomes Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

That person should have a taste of this.

“Take out the harpoon! Attack the next ship!”

“Yes!”

Slash!

Im So-Byeong swung his fan.

“Do not lose the momentum! Push and rush in right away! Archers! What are you doing? Shoot and kill those who are in the water!”

“Yes!”

As the whistle rang out, the archers on each ship began to shoot arrows.

“Kuak!”

“I-It’s the arrows! Dive in!”

“N-No!”

The current around them was extremely rough.

“Ahhh!”

Those who were pierced by Mount Hua’s swords let out desperate screams. Those whose necks were pierced died instantly, while those hit elsewhere had a tough time swimming.

The steep current swept them away, making it impossible to move properly and dragging them down.

“S-spare me!”

“Help me! Please!”

Jo Seung clicked his tongue as he watched the tragedy unfold on the river.

“If you look down on the river, things won’t work out for you.”

So, in order to cook and eat right, labor was needed.

“Um... Green Forest King.”

“What?”

Jo Seung spoke as if he felt bad.

“In my opinion, it would be better to place those who use spears on the edge of the ship.”

“Spears?”

“Yes... when people fall into the water, they cannot see anything, so they will climb up, not caring whether it’s the pirate ship or ours. Survival is their priority.”

“Hoo.”

Im So-Byeong looked at Jo Seung with shock.

“Nice opinion. Let’s do that.”

“Thank you!”

Jo Seung quickly bowed his waist.

Even though he didn’t care much about the Great Whale Pirates, Im So-Byeong was not to be underestimated. Wasn’t Im So-Byeong on the same level as the Black Dragon King, who rules the Yangtze River?

Just knowing that the Green Forest King accepted his opinion made him feel happy.

“You are pretty smart.”

“T-thank you.”

“The pirates seem smart too. You must have had a lot of trouble, right?”

“... well....”

“Are you interested in working for me?”

“Uh? Is that alright?”

“Oh, by the way, you weren’t given a choice. If you don’t come under my wing, there is a high chance you will be taken away by that guy and live your life in hell.”

At Im So-Byeong’s words, Jo Seung looked blankly. He saw Chung Myung rising above the ship, roaring and running wild.

“Uhahahaha! Die! Die, you bastard!”

“....”

Jo Seung turned his head again with an expressionless face and fell flat on the ground.

“I will accept it!”

“It is nice that you made the right decision. Then let us change clothes first. That guy isn’t good at remembering people’s faces, so he might beat you the moment he sees you.”

“....”

“Tsk tsk. It is because he is so vicious.”

A situation arose where the bandit leader was criticizing the Taoists for being vicious. Still, no one on the ship thought it was strange.

It was truly sad.

“R-retreat!”

“Go back to the base! He won’t be defeated here!”

The ship carrying the survivors began to turn its bow with all its might.

This was where an absurd scene of bandits fleeing to the land to avoid the bandits and mountain-dwelling Taoist people actually took place.

“Y-yah, you bastard!”

“Take us with you too!”

“Oh my...”

Those who were drowning and being showered with arrows looked at the ship moving away with despairing eyes. Retreat was something that could happen at any time if the tide was against you, but what kind of person would abandon and run away when nearly half of them were in the water?

“N-Now, we are dead for sure...”

There was no other way.

Those who climbed onto the enemy ship to survive were knocked down with spears and fell into the water. Now, there was only one choice: drown in the water or get hit by an arrow. It wasn’t known if there were people who managed to swim to the river’s edge, but it was probably only a small number.

At the very moment when despair fell into the eyes of the enemies.

“Are those bastards running away now?”

An evil and cruel voice exploded over the river.

“Chase! Go after them!”

“Be sure to start with the remnants...”

“It is obvious that we need to go after them! Why aren’t you moving fast?”

“I-I am coming. Let us leave these guys alone and chase after the ships! No! Stop aiming for them, you bastards! Chase after them!”

The folded oars came back, and the ship began to move ahead.

“It is said that if you fall behind, you die! Follow me!”

“Yes!”

The ship carrying the Green Forest bandits escaped between the sinking ships and began to move ahead at full speed. Meanwhile, the Green Forest bandits who had boarded the pirate ships also quickly got back onto their ship.

“Did you people eat slugs or what? Why are you so damn slow?”

“I-I am doing my best now!”

“Best? The only thing you people do is row and loot, and now you cannot even do that properly? And you want to live? Why would anyone help you out?”

“I-I am rowing at full speed now!”

“Hurry!”

The bandits rushed ahead and into the cabin. The pirate shouted as he grabbed the pole in the cabin and went down in one go.

“M-move quickly! Quick, you people! If he comes down, we will be beaten to death here!”

Who didn’t know who ‘he’ was?

“Eik!”

“Gosu hit those drums quickly! Now!”

Thud! Thud! Thud! Boom! Boom! Boom!

The drumming sound of the drummer sitting at the front of the room began to get faster. Then the people rowing the oars moved them faster and faster.

Rowing was harder labor than one could imagine, but since they were warriors who had learned martial arts, the speed was so fast that it was uncanny.

“Hurry! Faster!”

“Euhhh!”

“Kuaaaak!”

The fear even increased, and the pirates holding the oars rowed to the point of puking out blood. There was a repeated breaking sound from the oars, and the ship began to move out at an incredible speed.

“Nice!”

Chung Myung nodded with a satisfied expression. The distance to the fleeing ships was gradually narrowing.

“Let’s go!”

Chung Myung boarded the leading ship, drew his sword, and pointed it ahead.

Soon, the fleeing ships were seen anchoring, almost crashing into an island of reeds.

“Uh?”

Thud!

Woanngggg!

The ships that hit the sandbank made a thundering noise and went over.

“Yah! Wait! Reduce the speed! Slow down! Stop, you bastards!”

Of course, a ship that has gained speed cannot stop at once.

“We will crash!”

“Hold on tight! No, just jump out!”

‘Euhhhh! What are you doing, insane bastard!’

The oars were desperately lowered, but there was no way they could stop the ship.

Chung Myung smiled happily as he watched the ships getting closer at a high pace.

“Was it a bit much?”

KWAAANNNNNGGGGGG!

The ship with Chung Myung hit the back of the pirate ship and was literally destroyed.

“AHHH! You bastard!!”

The desperate voice of Jo Seung, who was unable to handle the force of the collision, soared into the air, and his shout echoed faintly over the flowing river.

Chapter 774: If You Fall Behind, You All Die! (4)

“Captain!”

“Eukk...”

Ga Nam-Pyung, the captain of Hidden Water’s Blue Base—a new pirate group that had dominated the island in the middle of the Yangtze River—frowned when he saw the ships floating up to the land.

The ships he had sent out were being driven away and were stuck on the sandbank. This meant there wasn’t even time to moderately slow down and stop the ship.

The pirate ships were following behind them at breakneck speed.

Defeat.

At least, they had no choice but to admit that they were completely defeated.

His men hurriedly jumped out of the tilting ship and crawled onto the land.

“Damn it.”

Anyone could see that these were the looks of defeated soldiers.

If the opponents were people well-versed in piracy or any water art, this defeat would be understandable. His subordinates weren’t well-trained either.

But weren’t the opponents just bandits?

It was like using the name ‘pirates’ vaguely, being chased by bandits who never even put their toes in water...

He had considered many situations while using this natural fortress as a base but never thought something this absurd would happen.

“W-what should I do?”

“What is that supposed to mean?”

Ga Nam-Pyung gestured to the ship with his chin.

“Yes, they are bandits. Catch them and kill them all!”

“B-But...”

As the subordinate looked at him with trembling eyes, Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes narrowed.

“If not? Are you planning to run from here? From this island?”

“....”

“It is the same whether we die by drowning or by fighting. If you really want to live, at least take down one more of their men!”

“Ah, I understand!”

The subordinate lowered his head and ran to the riverside with all his might. Ga Nam-Pyung snorted briefly at this.

“What idiots are these?”

“I guess it is only natural with so many people gathered together.”

Ga Nam-Pyung slightly turned his gaze at the voice he heard.

A man with a cold appearance, his whole body covered in a robe stained with black blood.

“Who was it that asked you to gather all those idiots together?”

“Who would have imagined that Green Forest would intervene?”

“Damn it!”

The person speaking to Ga Nam-Pyung was none other than the person who had previously attacked the Eunha special delivery person. The deep scars on his face were proof of it.

“What will you do now?”

“We fight.”

He spoke harshly.

“Or we leave everything and run.”

“...”

Ga Nam-Pyung gritted his teeth.

“Black-Haired Ghost, this wouldn’t have happened if you hadn’t acted impulsively, right?”

“What do you mean by impulsively? Making the pirates strong? Or attacking the special delivery person?”

The one called Black-Haired Ghost stuck out his tongue and licked his lips.

“Either way, you ultimately agreed to it. But now, it would be a little unfair to hold me solely responsible for this, right?”

“Cheeky bastard...”

Ga Nam-Pyung glared at the Black-Haired Ghost with snake-like eyes.

If anyone saw it, they would consider this a bizarre sight.

Pirates were basically a vertical power structure. As rough people gathered around, the ties between superiors and subordinates were often stricter than those in the Justice faction sects. But the current attitude of this Black-Haired Ghost towards the captain was no different from the attitude of a captain towards his subordinate.

“If all goes well, all the riches and glory will be yours. Right?”

“What do you even mean to say?”

“It means that with rights come responsibilities. If what you want to achieve is big, then what you need to endure will also be huge.”

“Haa!”

Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes turned cold in an instant. Just by looking at his eyes, it seemed like he would pull out his blade at any moment to attack the man.

“There is nothing to be so excited about. Because this is also a chance.”

“Chance?”

“Anyway, those people only fight in the mountains. They wouldn’t be used to battles like this. If we just bury all those people here, the reputation of the pirates will increase, and there will be more things to gain.”

“....”

“All we need to do is get past this crisis.”

“You speak so well.”

When the sarcastic answer came back, Black Haired-Ghost’s eyes narrowed for a moment.

“I hope you will stop being this perverse. Who is the person who rescued those White Thunder cannons, collected the gunpowder, and helped make the pirates? Who else handed over the position of captain for the sake of the waters which got created without any regret?”

“...”

“Do not forget. I am the one who showed favor to you.”

Kakak!

A sharp trident protruded from inside the Black Haired-Ghost’s sleeve. The man, clutching the three prongs, glanced at Ga Nam-Pyung and then turned.

“If you delay, you will miss the timing. We need to let them know that this is the most dangerous part.”

Ga Nam-Pyung stared intently, without blinking, at Black Haired-Ghost, who was heading towards the water without waiting for an answer. The sound of teeth-gritting came from his lips.

‘Just wait.’

If it wasn’t for the fame, he wouldn’t have the ability to even make a pirate group!

“Huh!”

Ga Nam-Pyung pulled out the blade from his belt.

It was almost like a sword rather than a blade. A horizontal blade with an edge on only one side of the straight iron without any bends.

“I will kill you all!”

Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes shone, and he ran to the waters.

“Rush!”

“Push away!”

“Kill them all!”

The ship carrying Chung Myung was the one that failed to slow down and crashed into the other ship. Still, there weren’t many idiots who would repeat the same thing after seeing that happen.

Of course, the fact that there wasn’t much doesn’t mean there wasn’t any, but... well, most of the ships slowed down and stopped safely at the edge.

Splash! Plop!

The Green Forest bandits jumped off the ship and rushed to the island.

“Ugh, guys? They seem prepared...”

“Ohhh!”

“Fight!”

“I will turn them into water ghosts today!”

Im So-Byeong, who was speechless, let out a deep sigh.

‘It is my turn.’

The art of war began with training soldiers who followed instructions. But unfortunately, the bandits here were the worst kind to follow such orders.

This meant there was a reason why the Green Forest King had simply and ignorantly destroyed the strategy with force.

In the first place, why would people who have the patience to receive instructions and follow them exactly as told come to deserted mountains and join bandits?

And this was a limitation of the Green Forest itself, which even the Green Forest warriors brought together for decades couldn’t escape.

And...

“Let’s go!”

“Ohhhhh!”

Chung Myung, who was at the forefront, waved his sword in the air to boost their morale.

“....”

Was it an illusion that the Green Forest bandits following behind him and repeated the cheers, looked several times more energetic than Im So-Byeong, who was in the lead?

“Okay!”

As Chung Myung attacked the fleeing enemies at the forefront, Mount Hua’s disciples followed, and the Green Forest bandits began to sweep away the weak ones like hungry tigers.

This was a disaster.

There was no other way to express this.

The pirates waiting on the island were frightened by the sight of the ship escaping and crashing into the sandbank. They were secondarily frightened by the sight of the bandits who rushed out of the ships.

In the meantime, could they come to their senses when their pursuers rushed at them with a force that could break down the mountains?

There was a difference in strength that made them wonder if they would even fight well against them, and with the losing momentum on their side, there was no way it would be a fight.

“Ahahahah! These bastards, how dare they touch Mount Hua?”

“We will make sure to make you pay for it!”

Jo Gul was taken aback by the voices coming from both sides and turned to see Yoon Jong.

“... aren’t those the bandits? Green Forest?”

“Who cares about that! Damn it! Just die, you pirate bastards!”

Jo Gul was shocked to see Yoon Jong running with his eyes closed.

“Uh... that reckless role is mine! S-sahyung! Let us go together!”

Gritting his teeth, Yoon Jong wielded his sword.

Paat!

His sword moved among the pirates more roughly than usual. Just as he had defeated several pirates in an instant and was about to seek his next target—

“You little brat!”

A loud roar came from behind. A man, whose attire was different from the other pirates, ran straight at Yoon Jong.

“I will rip you apart!”

Yoon Jong’s eyes gleamed with determination.

Tang!

Yoon Jong hit the ground and launched himself headfirst at the pirate.

“Huh!”

The pirate’s face showed bewilderment, clearly not expecting such a counter.

In a split second, Yoon Jong’s sword flew towards the pirate’s face like a beam of light.

“Tsk!”

Kakang!

The pirate’s blade barely managed to block Yoon Jong’s sword. But Yoon Jong’s sword, which bounced back faster, struck down the pirate’s blade again.

Kaang!

The powerful blow pushed the pirate’s blade back.

Yoon Jong’s sword then swung and cut the pirate’s chest horizontally.

“Kuaak....”

Thud!

Yoon Jong shouted loudly as he kicked the groaning pirate in the stomach.

“Defeat!”

“Woahh....”

“Come to your senses and fight right!”

“Yes, yes, sahyung!”

Jo Gul, regaining his composure, swung his sword.

Yoon Jong was probably very angry because these people had kidnapped civilians. Normally, this would result in a month-long nagging.

It was then.

“Sasuk! Take the lead! Sago! Please take the left side.”

“Okay!”

“Yes.”

“Soso! Stick behind Sasuk!”

“Okay!”

Chung Myung gestured around and shouted, looking at the Green Forest bandits.

“Push them!”

“Ohhh!”

The bandits rushed ahead with faces full of courage.

“Hmm.”

After carefully examining the situation, Chung Myung lowered his sword.

The battle had ended.

In large-scale battles, the side with the rising momentum will most likely win. In particular, in places where new terrain did not favor either side, the results were usually as good as full-strength battles.

There was no need for him to come ahead and take down the pirates in the hands of his sahyungs.

‘Just let them experience it all.’

Not everything he said at Mount Hua was empty words. In the past, just dragging them around was enough, but now that wasn’t sufficient. They needed to go through more and more experiences to learn.

‘That is right, but....’

Chung Myung was lost in thought for a moment and looked around with strange eyes.

‘It’s simpler than I thought, right?’

Although the scale of the island seemed large, neither the pirate fight that took place in the water nor now did it feel threatening to Chung Myung. The White Thunder cannons were a sure threat, but these people were nothing.

These people made such measly pirate groups and dared to touch Mount Hua?

“... is it because they are new?”

If one thought back, if this was a powerful pirate group, they would have backed out already. It would be even more strange if a newly formed pirate group did such a thing.

One could say that they were ignorant and thoughtless as they touched the civilians, but then they touched Mount Hua, and...?

‘Something doesn’t feel right?’

It was a moment when he thought he had to find the source of his discomfort.

“ACKKKKK!”

Chung Myung’s head suddenly rose at the loud scream from ahead. It was something different from the screams he heard before.

“What?”

At the place where the screams came from, a man half a foot taller than the others stood with a long blade. The bandits who tried to fight him were falling down, their bodies bloody.

The man shouted in anger.

“What are you doing! Turn back and run! But there is nowhere to run! If you don’t want to die, then fight me to the end!”

“Ohh?”

At a glance, he didn’t seem so special.

“Is that the captain?”

Well, no matter what one does, one needs to have the power to attract the attention of many.

Now then, who should he send...

Tat.

But before Chung Myung could even worry about it, someone jumped and landed in front of Ga Nam-Pyung.

“... uh?”

“Mount Hua’s Yu Yiseol.”

She spoke softly, aiming her sword at the captain’s neck.

“Will take that neck.”

Ga Nam-Pyung’s face contorted at that.

Chapter 775: If You Fall Behind, You All Die! (5)

“This insolent wench....”

Who wouldn’t be angry when a young swordswoman, who hadn’t even lived half the life the man did, said something so absurd?

Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes began to shine with killing intent.

“Let’s see if you can say the same thing after your body is destroyed.”

Cold tension flowed between the two.

“E-excuse me....”

Before they knew it, Jo Seung, who was standing close to Chung Myung, spoke in a trembling voice.

“Gre-great warrior. No, Taoist! I think we need to stop this.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung glanced at Jo Seung with eyes that seemed to ask, ‘What are you doing here?’ and tilted his head.

“Why?”

“That man is Ga Nam-Pyung. The captain! He is quite a famous warrior in the Yangtze River!”

“... Uh?”

“He uses the blade like a monster and is famous for his skill. He was asked to join various groups, but I heard that he turned them all down because he didn’t like working under someone... I never thought he would make his own pirate group...”

Jo Seung’s face was full of anxiety as he gulped.

“Ga Nam-Pyung, that man is on a whole different level from the young wandering pirates in the Yangtze River. In the past, when there was a fight with a warrior from one of the Nine Great Sects, he killed the person and also avoided being pursued by the sects. That is how dangerous and strong that man is.”

“Ah, is that so?”

“Yes! I wondered how the fledgling pirate group managed to attract so many.... It seems like there were people who wanted to join because of him. Ones who raised Ga Nam-Pyung separately.”

“Hmmm.”

Chung Myung looked at Ga Nam-Pyung with strange eyes.

“Well, he doesn’t seem that great, though.”

“I have my doubts about that too. As far as I know, he is a man with great strength, but he wasn’t known to be so resourceful... So when did he prepare to fight back like this...?”

“Does he have a lot of money?”

“Sorry?”

“Does he have a lot of money?”

“...what kind of money does a former vagrant have?”

“Is that so?”

Chung Myung’s eyes narrowed.

‘A vagrant who doesn’t have any money has so many White Thunder Cannons and manages to feed so many people?’

Only when the pirates started to run their own group would they be able to make a living with the money they had earned? But in the beginning, they would have to bring in money.

So, how did a newly formed pirate group manage to do all this?

“It doesn’t make sense unless he has a lot of money, right?”

Chung Myung tapped his cheek.

“Well, if you search for it, you can find it... rather, the land here seems strange. I thought it was a sandbank being called an island.”

“Taoist! Now isn’t the time to be looking at such things. She might die!”

“Who? Sago? Or the guy who is the thug or Ga Nam-Pyung?”

“...Uh?”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“You worry too much. Sago isn’t the kind of person to lose to someone like him who only does that.”

“Well, I think because you don’t know much about Ga Nam-Pyung...”

“Are you familiar with our Sago then?”

“...Sorry?”

“It’s the same as not knowing one another, right?”

Jo Seung’s face was filled with doubt at this. Chung Myung just shrugged his shoulders. Even though he seemed like he was bullshitting, it felt true.

“Taoist, are you really okay?”

“Okay?...uh?”

At that moment, Chung Myung tilted his head.

“Wait a minute.”

“Uh?”

“Are you the guy who ran away by cursing me earlier?”

“...Uh?”

“Haha. I almost forgot. Come here.”

“...”

Sometimes... good intentions came back with the worst results.

“Phew!”

Ga Nam-Pyung took a short breath and swung his blade.

Basically, the blade was closer to a weapon that swung and struck rather than cut. But the horizontal blade of Ga Nam-Pyung, which was similar to a sword, was great for stabbing.

Those who had mastered water arts must fight mindfully of the water. Heavy and thick blades weren’t great in water.

A heavy blade was suitable for a wanderer who often engaged in melee combat. At the same time, a weapon that could stab was suitable for a pirate who often fought in water.

And his weapon took both into consideration.

Swish!

Every time he moved it, the watchers felt their skin crawl at the clashing sound.

Ga Nam-Pyung, who thrust the sword at an incredible speed, thrust the blade once again. A dozen stabs were made in an instant. It was so clear and strong that it was hard to believe he was once a vagrant.

Making a name for yourself throughout the huge Yangtze River, which flowed through half the Central Plains, meant that his skills were good. And Ga Nam-Pyung’s blade clearly proved that his reputation wasn’t exaggerated.

But.

Wheik!

The extended horizontal blade couldn’t extend any further in front of Yu Yiseol. To a stranger, it might have seemed as if he had stopped right in front of her neck.

But, the expression of Ga Nam-Pyung was contorted while Yu Yiseol’s remained calm. It meant that things weren’t as they seemed.

‘This guy!’

Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes turned a little more cautious.

Just over halfway there.

Every time he extended the blade, Yu Yiseol took a step back, which was more than the distance his blade could reach. With a complete understanding of the path of the blade he was using, she was neutralizing his blade with minimal movement.

It would have been impossible to even try such a thing without complete confidence and boldness in the method they were training in.

The fact that she was showing such composure towards him upset him even more.

But rather than getting angry, he took a deep breath. Losing one’s rational mind on the battlefield was more dangerous than sticking one’s head on the pirates’ blade.

‘That’s right, she’s still a young one with just a little experience.’

With just this one move, he could understand the opponent’s skills. If they fought ten times, he would win at most a couple of times.

But wasn’t it experience and skill that made it possible to achieve those two wins with a 100 percent chance?

Swish!

Ga Nam-Pyung’s blade once again aimed for Yu Yiseol’s throat.

With light footwork, Yu Yiseol took half a step back from where the blade reached.

Wheik! Swish!

Sounds that seemed meaningless poured out one after another.

A series of ear-splitting noises and rapid, invisible stabs. It was a rough and flashy attack, but it seemed to lack force.

‘Hmm?’

At that moment, Chung Myung, who was watching the scene, smiled strangely.

“You’re showing some tricks.”

As if he had heard those words, Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes lit up, and he extended the blade again. This time, Yu Yiseol leaned back as if it were natural.

However, at that moment, Ga Nam-Pyung’s blade, which should have stopped in the middle, seemed to move further and suddenly rushed as if it would pierce Yu Yiseol’s neck. Her eyes widened, and she jumped back without delay.

“Tat!”

But Ga Nam-Pyung didn’t let her get away so quickly. The sharp sword movement flew back to her neck at incredible speed.

Yu Yiseol swung her sword to block the path.

Gagagagak!

The blade qi that collided with her blade moved to the side, making the sound of scraping metal.

Crack!

Although the injury was avoided, the qi in the blade was enough to leave a dull pain in Yu Yiseol’s wrist.

Paat!

Ga Nam-Pyung rushed ahead without giving her time to spare. Then, he kicked the sand below his feet. The sand flew up, covering Yu Yiseol and preventing her from moving.

“Tsk.”

A look of shock appeared on Yu Yiseol’s face.

And Baek Cheon, who saw that, was equally shocked.

‘What? Why did samae....?’

Of course, the guy called Ga Nam-Pyung wasn’t weak, but right now, it wasn’t like Yu Yiseol was overwhelmed by his skills either. However, she was unable to show off her skills for some reason.

It was evident from the flustered look on Yu Yiseol’s face, which usually looked calm in any situation.

“The sense of distance.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon asked back without even looking at Chung Myung, who had spoken.

“The sound coming from the hole at the end of the blade. It is a sound technique. If one continues to listen to that sound, it will shake their insides.”

“....”

“And that guy has been adjusting the distance he extends the blade from earlier. It is confusing to the eyes and ears at the same time. That man is a lot smarter than we thought, right?”

The same went for kicking up the sand.

Ga Nam-Pyung, too, wouldn’t think that someone as talented as Yu Yiseol would be able to handle such tactics. But what if they thought about all such changes accumulating? If they constantly shook their ears and confused their vision, one would mistake the entire thing.

“Nasty...”

“There is nothing nasty about fighting. It is 100 times better to live a miserable life than to live a clean life.”

“...”

Baek Cheon nodded silently. Although he reflexively criticized it, he had no choice but to agree with it as someone who had experienced countless battles.

“How is it? Shouldn’t we help her out?”

“Don’t speak nonsense.”

When Chung Myung asked vaguely, Baek Cheon answered firmly.

“It isn’t that big of a deal.”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung smiled with his lips.

“How is that?”

Ga Nam-Pyung roared like a tiger. This wasn’t evidence of excitement in the battle. This was the booming voice to confused Yu Yiseol in the fight.

There were two ways to win against a better opponent: either become stronger or bring the opponent down to the same level as oneself.

Attaching the bells to the hole of the blade and moving the blade at different distances were all martial arts techniques from the ancient history of the Evil Faction.

The man’s blade arts could be considered a development of such martial arts. Every time the blade moved, it carried internal qi and sounds that resonated with the natural Tao.

If one had experience observing the martial arts of the Evil Faction, they would find a way to counter it. However, the young swordsman ahead couldn’t have experienced all that.

Moreover, if it was natural for her to attack with clean strikes, she would be taken aback when the blade moved differently.

As proof...

Pat!

The blade grazed a part of Yu Yiseol’s arm just below the shoulder. The torn clothes soon turned red with blood.

Pat!

Another wound appeared on her side.

The blade, which hadn’t even been touching her, was now clearly causing wounds on her body.

‘A bit more.’

Basically, you have to be careful when hunting.

In times like this, if one became excited, they might make a clumsy attack or fall into trouble. Time was on his side now, so he could proceed slowly.

‘I will kill you first!’

Ga Nam-Pyung’s eyes held killing intent.

“Ahhh!”

It was the moment when Ga Nam-Pyung threw his blade down, channeling all his qi into it.

Kaang!

Before the blade could reach its full extension, it collided with Yu Yiseol’s sword.

“Tsk!”

However, Ga Nam-Pyung didn’t panic and calmly retrieved his sword and threw it again.

Kang!

But it was the same this time.

Before he could fully swing it, Yu Yiseol’s sword intercepted the path and stopped it.

Kaang! Kaang! Kang! Kang!

No matter how many times he extended the path of the blade, the same thing happened each time. Before his sword was even halfway extended, Yu Yiseol stretched out her sword and parried it away as if she knew his moves.

Ga Nam-Pyung, who became scared and stepped back, looked at Yu Yiseol with shocked eyes. She spoke with a face that didn’t care again.

“I kind of understand it.”

“....”

“Sound, distance, feel. It is a meaningful thing when the blade is extended till the end alone.”

“W-what...”

So, she was saying she wouldn’t let him do a full swing?

Did that make sense?

For such a thing to be possible, the woman’s blade should be twice as fast as Ga Nam-Pyung’s sword.

A Delayed Movement.

This means that the sword, extended later after the attack, flew a greater distance than his sword.

“S-something like that cannot happen! Ah!”

Ga Nam-Pyung let out a scream and stabbed his blade again. This noise was not a sane one. It was a scream borne of fear.

Kaang! Kang! Kang!

But his blade didn’t move along the path till the end. Every time he stabbed the blade, the sword flew like a ghost and bounced it back.

He was pulling out all the qi he had and stabbed the blade faster, but the sword was still ahead of him.

“Ahh!”

Now, Ga Nam-Pyung’s movements as he thrust the blade looked close to a fit.

If she blocked it, then just push it down with force!

There was no need to fall behind in strength. This young woman...

At that moment.

Rotation.

Yu Yiseol’s body rotated and gently let Ga Nam-Pyung’s blade flow to the side.

Then, it swam lightly like a butterfly and stretched out the sword.

The world flowed slowly.

Ga Nam-Pyung, who sensed his fate, realized one thing then.

‘No... uh?’

There was no sign of the Black-haired Ghost.

There was no sign of the Black-haired Ghost, who had joined the battle before him, anywhere on the battlefield.

‘W-where is he?’

He wasn’t there.

A person with skills like his should be noticed. But neither he nor his subordinates who joined him were around.

“This fucking....”

Swish.

But before he could even curse him right, Yu Yiseol’s sword cut through his throat.

Slash!

Ga Nam-Pyung’s head rose into the air without any noise.

His head saw the world spinning.

Across the island.

The sight of a group of black-dressed people leaving the waters of the Yangtze River and moving from the island.

‘I was fooled....’

The world went dark quickly.

Anger and hatred belong to the living.

Ga Nam-Pyung no longer had the right to be angry.

Chapter 776: If You Fall Behind, You All Die! (6)

Tak!

The sound of a severed head hitting the sandbank was eerie.

It wasn’t loud, but it lingered among those fighting on the land.

“C-captain...”

“Uh...”

The captain was dead.

And it wasn’t at the hands of the guy jumping onto the ships earlier, but someone else. The implications of that fact were clear.

‘W-we cannot win.’

‘Damn it, I didn’t want this to happen.’

Basically, the people in such places who existed to plunder were either from the mountains or the water, people with poor skills, people who didn’t fit elsewhere, or people who committed a crime and ran away.

They had no loyalty to those around them, so they had no will to fight if their captain was dead. Only fear and confusion spread around.

The pirates’ eyes were filled with desperation and despair.

Sensing the atmosphere was right, Chung Myung smiled and stepped forward.

“It is all done...”

“Drop your weapons. Whoever surrenders will be spared.”

“...”

Chung Myung opened his eyes and looked around.

Before he knew it, Baek Cheon stepped forward and shouted.

“Those who resist should be prepared to sacrifice their lives! I will not warn you twice! Drop the weapons!”

The pirates trembled at the solemn, scolding voice and looked at each other.

Actually, the death of the captain was merely symbolic. Regardless of whether he was alive or dead, the outcome had already been decided. The odds of them winning were non-existent in the first place.

When they realized this, they quickly threw away their weapons and fell flat on the floor.

“What? You want to spare them again?”

“Just kill them! What good will living with these bastards even do!”

Then, a strange situation occurred where the bandits complained for the pirates to be killed.

If it were Chung Myung, he would have scolded the bandits first, but Baek Cheon was a person who knew basic manners to help them.

“I want to avoid unnecessary killing.”

“Hmm... well, you are a Taoist after all.”

The bandits nodded in agreement.

In the first place, they were simply there to provide support. They hadn’t achieved anything significant enough to order him around. As for the previous happenings, it was almost all due to Chung Myung.

“Drop the weapons, you bastards!”

“What? Is that guy rebelling?”

Most surrendered right away, but some fought till the end.

If it had been Mount Hua’s disciples who had surrounded them, they might have tried to subdue them without killing them. But now, it was the bandits who surrounded them. There was no way they would show mercy to those who had wanted to kill them just moments ago.

“Kuak!”

“Ack!”

In an instant, those who were pierced by the sword fell down, screaming to their deaths. Only after seeing that scene did those who held onto their weapons until the end hurriedly surrender.

“Hmm.”

Baek Cheon’s mouth hardened as he saw the scene.

Really, it was so simple.

It had certainly been difficult for them, but from the time Chung Myung arrived, it was cleared in less than a day.

‘It is so frustrating.’

Such an incredibly simple task...

No, actually, if they thought about it calmly, it wasn’t a simple matter. If all of this had been simple, would there have been any reason for the Nine Great Sects to sit back and watch?

It was all going backward.

This wasn’t a simple matter but was handled simply because of Chung Myung.

Looking back, what they experienced was all difficult and tough.

Fighting with Southern Edge ties with the Sichuan Tang family, visiting the Beast Palace, and fighting the Demonic Sect in the North Sea.

‘We were able to do it because of him.’

He thought he had understood it already. But, looking back, Baek Cheon and the other Mount Hua disciples just focused on the strength aspect of Chung Myung and not his resourcefulness.

“Why do you look so sullen?”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon turned at the sound he heard. Chung Myung was looking at him, puzzled.

“I won.”

“... ah, right.”

When Baek Cheon hesitantly answered, Chung Myung secretly chuckled.

‘He must have felt it.’

Developing skills was a good thing, and it was more important than anything else.

However, the most important thing in practice was to create a situation where they could fully demonstrate the skills they developed. The Five Swords’ biggest mistake this time wasn’t coming to Yangtze, nor was it moving hastily and giving the enemies the victory.

It was being dragged into a situation where they couldn’t demonstrate their skills.

‘And this was my mistake too.’

As Chung Myung watched this situation, he keenly felt the meaning of Hyun Jong’s words.

The existence of Chung Myung was a great fortune for them, but on the other hand, it was also a huge obstacle for them.

-No, you moron! If you have an idea, say it and move! Or at least use your common sense. That is why you always move alone and stab around! If you go insane every time something happens, what happens to the person following you?

-You are too noisy, brat!

Tang Bo also grumbled in such times.

Even Tang Bo, who was an elder in the Tang family, found it tough to follow Chung Myung through the battlefield. Then how bad was it for the Mount Hua disciples, whose skills weren’t even on par with Tang Bo’s, much less their lesser experience?

Learning only had meaning when one understood it. If they were dragged around without understanding, they would not learn and develop understanding.

First of all, finding and confirming the enemy and finding the optimal method for the situation on the scene was a favorite for Chung Myung. Baek Cheon also saw and felt things in his own manner, but unfortunately, Baek Cheon and the other Five Swords are not Chung Myung.

This was what happened when one imitated Chung Myung’s method of finding the flow while fighting numerous battles in the head.

The situation would have been much better if Baek Cheon had been as careful as he usually was. This time, he moved with motivation, causing anger.

Since Chung Myung knew the entire process, he didn’t blame the Five Swords.

‘Failure is painful, but... in the end, we benefit.’

As long as one survived and didn’t die, all experiences became assets. This failure would become their trainer for a better future.

And for them, it was a necessary failure.

‘Because from now on, they will have to lead and fight.’

Chung Myung’s eyes were sharp.

Until now, they had been able to fight on their own. But from now on, the scale of battles they will experience will grow larger. There will be more instances of them leading others to fight in places where Chung Myung isn’t present.

If they had failed too late, the damage would have been too great. They were fortunate to have been able to experience and reflect on it in advance.

“Ugh, sasuk.”

“Yes?”

“Everything must end right now. It isn’t fully done, right?”

“...”

Baek Cheon, who was staring at Chung Myung, nodded.

“You are right.”

Then he looked back at the sinkhole in the back and said,

“We need to finish cleaning up.”

He gathered the disciples of Mount Hua right then.

“First, rescue the civilians caught in the ditch there. There might be people whose bodies are exhausted. Help them, Soso.”

“Yes, sasuk!”

“And check to see if the missing disciple of the Ghost Clan is here. Some might have been taken as prisoners. Baek Sang, interrogate the pirates to see if there are any hostages around.”

“I understand, sahyung.”

“Okay.”

Baek Cheon nodded at the quick response.

‘We had won.’

Always producing results that exceeded their abilities. At some point, it seemed like he started to believe the results were all his own doing.

He pretended to be humble, but he wasn’t really humble.

“The biggest mistake isn’t losing or making mistakes. It is not learning from the defeats and mistakes.”

“....”

“I will reflect on them myself. So, don’t forget what you all have learned from this, and keep it in mind.”

“Yes.”

As the sajaes nodded with serious eyes, Baek Cheon had a faint smile.

“Ugh, pretending to be cool?”

“...”

Because of a grumpy voice that came from behind, the seriousness faded fast.

“Phew,”

After swimming for a long time until the island was barely visible, the Black-Haired Ghost walked to the land.

The island they left was barely visible now.

“The battle has ended, right?”

One of the subordinates who followed him glanced behind and asked.

“I guess so.”

“Then, surely the captain must have died too.”

The Black-Haired Ghost wiped his drenched face with his sleeve and smiled.

“That idiot. He probably didn’t even have any doubts till the end.”

“How could he have suspected anything? Isn’t it true that we brought those expensive cannons and ships to offer them?”

“... that is a shame though.”

White Thunder cannons and ships were things that couldn’t be purchased with money alone. It was such a waste that those things sank to the bottom now.

But it didn’t matter. Because they weren’t even his, to begin with.

“I didn’t know. But this was bound to happen.”

Black-Haired Ghost clicked his tongue and shook his head.

When he thought about what went into making that pirate group, it was like pouring raw pigs into the place. It was difficult for him to understand what this was.

“I just need to get paid for the services.”

“True.”

Hearing a voice other than his subordinate, Black-Haired Ghost suddenly turned his head. A person was walking out from the forest around the river.

Black-Haired Ghost, who was looking with slightly wary eyes, opened his mouth quietly.

“There was no need for you to come here to meet...”

“I came here because it seemed like things were moving more quickly than I thought.”

“There is nothing to worry about. I have done as instructed.”

“Right.”

The scholar-like man, who was facing the Black-Haired Ghost, nodded with a cold face.

“Do you need confirmation?”

“Is there any need for it?”

He took something out of his sleeve and held it out to the Black-Haired Ghost.

“It is here.”

The Black-Haired Ghost took the paper the man held out.

“It is a confirmed paper issued by the Finance Hall. I sent the money to the place you specified. If one combines it with the advance payment I gave earlier, it will be the exact amount promised.”

“... I am sure.”

Nodding his head, the Black-Haired Ghost took the paper and folded it.

“You can handle it yourself.”

“You don’t know what will happen in this world, right? A fool like me has no choice but to be careful about everything. I hope you understand.”

“It isn’t that I don’t understand. Like you said, one has to be careful in everything.”

The man nodded slightly and continued to speak.

“Then our deal is done.”

“Yes, thank you.”

“What is your plan now?”

“As promised, I will wash my hands and not set foot in Kangho. I plan to take my money and go to a faraway land or spend the rest of my life in a suitable place.”

“You are credible.”

“People might not believe that, but it is better to be sure of things.”

“Which is why I came to you.”

A faint smile appeared on the lips of the Black-Haired Ghost.

“It is no use deceiving people like that, but I think I received too much money.”

“It was well worth it.”

“Maybe... can I ask why you did this?”

The handsome-looking man put his finger on his lip.

“Your mouth invites danger. There are some things in the world better not known.”

“... I will forget it. No, I forgot it.”

“Right. Well, then, you had a rough time. Good luck.”

“Yes. Bye...”

It was at that moment,

Swish!

Dozens of rain drops poured down at incredible speed from the forest, where the men came out and landed on the necks of those standing.

“Kuak!”

“Ack!”

Those shocked at this clutched their necks and fell to the ground.

“...”

The Black-Haired Ghost looked down at his chest with a shocked look. His chest also had a short hole in it, caused by a dagger that hit him.

He slowly raised his head to see the man in front of him and opened his mouth in a trembling voice.

“Why...”

“Didn’t I tell you?”

The man spoke in an emotionless style.

“It is better to be certain in everything. But is there any reason for me to leave you alone?”

“... m-money is....”

“It is different from that. Of course, I don’t doubt you. I sent the money, too. And you don’t have to worry about your family getting it either. I promise on my name to not touch them. Your family gets to live a comfortable life.”

“...”

The Black-Haired Ghost fell to the ground. He knelt down and took deep breaths.

“W-why me of...”

“That is because this is much more important than you think. No one should know. Right, no one. The cost isn’t as bad as I feared. If you think about the price of a life, that is.”

“...”

“Rest now.”

Thud.

As the man fell, the handsome man spoke.

“Cut off the head and bury the bodies separately, and throw the body into the river.”

“Yes, military head!”

The man.

Ho Ga-Myung of the Ten Thousand People clan made a slightly complicated look at the distant island.

“Pirates...”

Soon, a smile fell on his lips.

“Once the seeds are sown, harvest it. Thanks to Mount Hua, things will get a lot easier now.”

Ho Ga-Myung turned around.

“Let us head back before we get caught. Hurry up!”

“Yes!”

A moment passed as such.

There were no traces left on the riverside.

Only the river continued to flow.

Chapter 777: Life Is Innately Painful (1)

“Thank you! Thank you!”

“Oh my... oh my, Taoist, I survived thanks to you Taoists!”

“How do I repay this favor...?”

Yoon Jong shook his head.

“Favor? It isn’t something like that. We did what we had to do.”

“Thank you so much. But we need to repay you somehow!”

“It is really fine...”

“Woah! Repay? It is good to repay! Who said repay! Repay!”

Chung Myung pushed Yoon Jong aside and grabbed the hand of the old man in front of him.

“You said repay, right?”

“Uh? Ah... yes...”

Yoon Jong’s face contorted.

“It would have been enough to remove the pirates, but you even bothered to help us...!”

“Then, when you get out of here, spread the word about the Mount Hua sect defeating the pirates and saving you all.”

“... Sorry?”

“Rumors, rumors! That we saved you! All you have to do is tell the truth as well, saying the two pirate groups got destroyed! Then it will be a great reward, right?”

“Well, is that alright?”

“Oh my, that is truly a great reward. Instead, you have to be really proactive. You understand, right?”

“If it helps, I will do my best.”

“Kuak. He is someone who understands it all.”

Yoon Jong, who was trying to scold Chung Myung, fell silent.

Because this wasn’t even a reward.

“Then what about us?”

“First, we will check those in weak condition, and if they are alright, we will send them to the land right away.”

“L-land....”

“Ah, don’t worry, go to the nearest port. Or would you like to wait and go to where you originally wanted?”

“Well, I cannot ask you to take up so much trouble.”

“W-what? I don’t even drive a ship.”

“Uh?”

“Hehe. Just a joke.”

Chung Myung giggled and laughed.

The old man held his hand with a bewildered look.

“We sent all the civilians on the ship.”

Jo Gul asked with an anxious face after hearing Yoon Jong’s words.

“Would it be alright? It is a ship driven by pirate people, right?”

“We sent the Green Forest bandits with them, so it should be alright.”

Something sounded quite wrong here.

It was difficult to pinpoint, but there was surely something off about this.

Wanderers among pirates...

And he turned his face to Jo Seung.

“... This is my first time hearing this. If they had money, why not?”

“Well, right.”

There was something strange about this situation.

‘No, it isn’t a good situation.’

Well, they had all been wiped out, so whatever they did now was meaningless. What was important was that those who messed with Mount Hua’s friends were punished properly, and that would give a clear warning to other pirates.

“Now we clean up and leave.”

Baek Cheon looked at the wide Yangtze River with a slightly relaxed expression.

There was a lot of work involved, but it was a journey where he learned a lot.

“I don’t even want to see the water anymore, sahyung.”

“When I return to Mount Hua, I won’t even wash my face for a while.”
