Mount Hua’s disciples regained their spirits.

They had many things to reflect on, so they couldn’t head back feeling down.

‘If you fall, get up and run again.’

Baek Cheon composed himself and spoke.

“Then let’s end this... but, where is Chung Myung?”

“Huh? Wasn’t he here?”

“... where did he go? Don’t you feel anxious when such a thing happens?”

The disciples of Mount Hua tilted their heads and looked around. The land was wide and flat, so he couldn’t possibly hide around. They soon found Chung Myung standing on the edge of the island and approached him.

“What are you doing here?”

“Sasuk.”

Chung Myung, standing at the edge of the long island and looking towards the land on the other side, spoke without turning his head.

“How far is it from here to there?”

“Huh?”

Although the question was shocking, Baek Cheon answered.

“Maybe... 100 feet? No, around 300 feet?”

He realized now how wide the Yangtze River was.

Usually, it is called a river even if it was 100 feet wide, but 300 feet was too big.

Even this was just the distance from the island in the middle, so it must be enormous. Didn’t it mean that the actual river was twice as wide as this?

“Is it close?”

“Huh?”

Close?

“I understand why the pirates left this place alone for so long. This place is too close to the land. One can get here and get out fast.”

“Don’t you think this concept of movement is strange, though?”

Wasn’t 300 feet big enough? Across the water at that?

“It is so conspicuous when it comes to attacking from land. It only takes 300 people to cross them, right? At least, it is worth holding on to with the rapid waves around, but if one looks at this objectively, it is easy to be attacked from the land.”

“Listening to you...”

Baek Cheon looked around at Chung Myung’s words, his face contorting.

“No, that means if we had moved by land along the river and then took the ship here, we could have crossed this and attacked them. Then why did we come by ship? The damn motion sickness hit everyone...!”

“Ah, not important now.”

Chung Myung smiled.

“How’s it?”

“Uh?”

“Isn’t it nice? So close to land, an ambiguous place to attack by ship.”

“What is so nice about this?”

“If we just occupy this place, won’t it be difficult for the pirates to approach?”

“... Uh?”

Wait. Why was this brat speaking so much?

Occupy?

“... Chung Myung?”

“The more I look at it, the better it seems. Those little people are stupid and settled here to do their pirating, but from our side, this place is a bad spot.”

“... You, what are you saying?”

“Let’s have it here!”

“....”

Baek Cheon stared at Chung Myung and then looked back. Indeed, the sajaes behind him had the same confused faces.

‘Right, I am not the only one confused, right?’

Baek Cheon smiled and shouted.

“Yeah, you insane bastard! Why do we have to be here? This isn’t Shaanxi! It is the Yangtze River!”

“Is there any law about us not being here? Those bastards also came here to set up a pirate group, right?”

“... There is no law about it, but...”

“Do not get so easily convinced, sasuk!”

“Don’t interfere!”

“R-right!”

Baek Cheon nodded, looking lost, but he quickly came to his senses and asked.

“What do we do after taking this place? You want to try and rob people?”

“Tsk, tsk. You cannot seem to get your head around the concept.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung frowned disapprovingly.

“Why did we come here in the first place?”

“Uh... it is because the special deliveries of Eunha were attacked.”

“Is there any guarantee something like that won’t happen again?”

“...”

Chung Myung pointed to Jo Seung.

“Once is tough, but doing it twice will be easy. As soon as the rumors spread about the special delivery escorts being attacked, everyone will do so. And all the troubles sasuk went through till now will be in vain.”

“... the Great Whale pirates were a larger group than this. It is a bit...”

“They are pirates.”

“...”

Jo Seung, who spoke with an unfair feeling, was struck down with a blow and went silent.

“If something like this happens again, are you going to run all the way to the Yangtze River again to fight them? Then, you will have to run without wasting time to get here, right?”

“... Yes.”

As long as the enemy were pirates, there was a chance of the Yangtze River transport route always being hit. It was a risk that could not be avoided. Moreover, the escorts carried expensive items due to their nature, so anyone would want them.

On flat ground, they could avoid danger with quick feet, but wasn’t it different on ships?

“So we stay here?”

“If things are going to happen like that, wouldn’t it be better for us to take this land? Look here. That side and this side are close. So I am saying the problem can be solved by just bringing in two ships to move back and forth from the mainland to the island, right?”

Baek Cheon looked at the sajaes, asking them to do something.

Then Jo Gul, reading that, spoke.

“For a base, this isn’t a bad location!”

“Yah, you crazy bastard! How can you agree to it!”

“N-No. I don’t get what is so wrong?”

“Are you saying this just for the special deliveries?”

“Then the other merchants can also use it. They can just pay the ship toll and move.”

As expected of the youngest son of a merchant family. Why was his calculation only quick when it came to money?

“No! Never! It is too easy to be attacked, you said it too. What will you do if pirates rush in?”

“What can they do on a ship? We will be on land here, right?”

“Uh?”

“If the pirates come too, they are third-rate warriors at best. We won’t lose in a land battle.”

“...”

Baek Cheon was starting to be persuaded.

“And there are good things too.”

“Good things?”

“White Thunder cannons.”

“...”

“They will be plundered soon, and we won’t move in water. They can be placed on the water’s edge on land and shoot the pirates when they come. They cannot even approach us.”

“N-No...”

Baek Cheon closed his eyes tightly.

‘We are ruined.’

The nonsense was starting to sound plausible now.

“B-basically! We cannot be stuck here doing that!”

“Has sasuk lost it?”

“Uh?”

“Why will we do it? It isn’t just 1-2 people around here. Take this chance and tell the Green Forest to advance to the Yangtze River. They can split the toll in half with us.”

“...”

Yah... at this point, he felt like Chung Myung had planned ahead.

Baek Cheon was biting his nails, nervous because he couldn’t find a way to attack with logic.

“I am feeling a bit anxious.”

“Uh?”

Yu Yiseol, who was the sane person among them, expressed an opposing opinion.

“The range of the cannon. The breadth of the river. If a ship occupies the middle of the river, that will be isolated, too. Too far also.”

“Yes, yes! Even so, 300 feet is impossible to hit!”

“Then we reduce it to 200.”

“Uh? Using what?”

“Bridge.”

“... on water?”

“Yes.”

“Here?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung smiled brightly and nodded.

“Yah, you insane bastard. It cannot be done right on land, and you want to do that on water! At least speak sensible things! I have never heard of a bridge made on the Yangtze River!”

“Eh. There is no need to make a full bridge. And no need to make it right. Just throw things left and right. Just so the ships cannot move too much.”

“Uh?”

“Anchor the ships around and build a bridge on them. All we need to do is connect the ships to make it a bridge, place the cannons on top of it, and use it for docking.”

“...”

“Then we can shoot arrows and cannons from the bridge, and if there are people too, we can fight them! Ugh! I think this is awesome! As far as I know, this is a strategy thought up by a famous tactician long ago. So it can be true.”

... All the people on the ship get hit by arrows and are burned to death! That insane bastard! That place isn’t even far from here! But it is on a river!

“Hehehe. The more I think about it, the more amazing it gets. These bastards have brought us everything we need! We need to thank them!”

“Ch-Chung Myung!”

“Let’s start by getting a ship here! Hehehe! This seems fun, right?”

“Chung Myung...”

“Yah! You insane person! Where are you going?”

Chung Myung had no intention of listening to others; he was running alone.

Someone placed a hand on Baek Cheon’s shoulder as he was about to move.

“... monk?”

Hae Yeon had a bright smile.

“Amitabha, Taoist Baek Cheon. You know?”

“... what?”

“Life is innately full of suffering.”

“...”

Really... Thank you.

Yes.

Chapter 778: Life Is Innately Painful (2)

White smoke continued to flow from the small incense burner. As a result, the room was filled with pure white smoke, so much so that one couldn’t even see what was ahead.

There was a slow, drowsy breathing sound and a strong smell of alcohol.

A strange atmosphere that didn’t seem to belong to the dimly lit room.

Step. Step. Step.

At that moment, determined footsteps, which also didn’t match the atmosphere, were heard from the outside, and then a short, cold voice came through the door.

“Clan leader. I have returned after completing the mission.”

No answer came.

The person who had been waiting and remaining silent for a long time gave a brief command.

“Open.”

“But the clan leader is....”

“Open it.”

“... Uh?”

When the lightly closed door opened wide, the pure white smoke that filled the room rushed out.

Ho Ga-Myung slightly frowned at the scent, which irritated the tip of his nose.

“Hmm.”

After the smoke cleared a bit, he saw several people sleeping in the room, all spread out in different directions. Amid the spilled alcohol and unconscious people, a small brazier continued to give out smoke.

And in the center, on top of a large golden chair, which was somewhat unnaturally placed, was a person he knew very well, asleep.

Ho Ga-Myung sighed.

“Get up.”

Those who were lying down didn’t even move, as if they hadn’t heard his words.

“Let’s see if you still sleep after the head falls off.”

Did they hear that?

One by one, they looked up and saw Ho Ga-Myung standing in front of the door, and they turned thoughtful with fear.

“Oh, commander...”

“Get lost.”

“Yes!”

They all hurriedly got up, scared, and rushed out. As they desperately tried to move their drowsy bodies, barely sobering up, they rolled on the ground, tangled with one another. It was a mess.

After seeing them rush out of the room, Ho Ga-Myung sighed and ordered,

“Clean the furnace.”

“Yes, commander.”

Those who followed him entered the room and carefully carried the furnace out.

Then, as if he had done this numerous times before, he cleared the floor and opened the window to provide ventilation.

“Done.”

“Yes!”

As soon as Ho Ga-Myung finished speaking, they all bowed deeply and left the room in perfect order.

Finally, Ho Ga-Myung came inside and stood in front of the golden throne.

“Clan leader.”

“...”

“Clan leader.”

“Um?”

Jang Ilso, who was lying on the gold bed-like chair, looked up softly and slowly raised his head. Then he frowned and looked around.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, clan leader.”

“It is cold. Close the door.”

Jang Ilso covered his body with the blanket cleanly. A deep sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s mouth.

“Clan leader, if you keep doing this in broad daylight, your dignity as clan leader will not be maintained.”

“... You start nagging the moment you arrive.”

“That is why...”

“You talk like you don’t know me. I hate being bored. Even though I know I have to wait, I don’t like waiting.”

“You need to wake up.”

“Okay.”

Jang Ilso stopped babbling, pulled the blanket aside, and sat up as if sleeping was no longer an option with Ho Ga-Myung around.

Then, the servants waiting outside began to step in cautiously. The first servant who came in carefully offered a drink with both hands, and Jang Ilso took it to his mouth.

“You cannot be too absorbed in dreaming.”

“Fine. Fine.”

“I am not saying this out of concern for the clan leader. What can that smoke even do to the clan leader? But the other kids are different.”

“Fine, okay? You are just nagging me more and more.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand as if in disgust.

Then Ho Ga-Myung lowered his head as if he felt bad.

In the meantime, the servants stood by the side of Jang Ilso and diligently groomed him. They soaked the silk in clean water from the basin, wiped Jang Ilso’s face, and combed his disheveled hair.

The servants then began to wipe his eyelids with silk.

“It hurts. Be careful.”

“C-clan leader! I-I-I am sorry...”

At that moment, as the servant’s face turned pale and their body trembled, Jang Ilso sighed as if he were shocked.

“Why are you shaking so much? Why? Is something going to eat you?”

“I-I made a grave mistake! Forgive me just this once...”

“...you a new one?”

“Yes?”

Jang Ilso sighed heavily and shook his head.

“I don’t get why the newly selected people get sent here with the wrong information. It’s like clearing the throat also calls for a war here.”

“What could they have heard? They must have heard what it was.”

“Then how can you be scared? Kid, I am a caring person.”

“Yes! Yes, clan leader. Thank you...”

Jang Ilso smiled lightly and patted the head of the servant, who was shedding tears of joy at being alive. Then he slowly opened his arms. The servants waiting behind took off the white robe he was wearing.

“...”

Ho Ga-Myung looked at that and let out a low breath.

A truly perfect body.

There was no other way to explain Jang Ilso’s bare body, which they rarely saw because it was covered in layers of silk clothing; other than that, it was perfect.

The slightly thin body was harmonious as if someone had sculpted it, and the solid muscles built into the body without traces of fat impressed Ho Ga-Myung, who had seen countless warriors. No matter how many times he saw this, he always thought it was amazing.

But unfortunately, no one who sees this body for the first time would pay attention to such things. No, they wouldn’t even be interested in seeing it.

Before that, their eyes would be drawn to the scars perfectly placed on the body.

At first glance, it looked like dozens of black snakes were entangled together, covered in scars. Scars that resembled the dents or claws of an animal. Scars from being pierced by something sharp. That was all. Cut by the sword, by the blade, torn by hand...

Every type of scar that could exist in the world was now on his body.

Jang Ilso, whose body bore scars that looked like intentional tattoos, blinked his drowsy eyes and looked at Ho Ga-Myung.

The pressure was so intense that Ho Ga-Myung, who had served the man for over ten years, stopped breathing for a moment.

The true face of Jang Ilso, the Hegemony Lord, was hidden behind the gorgeous silk clothes.

The thorny path that Jang Ilso had walked until now was engraved on his body like scars. This was the path taken by him, who won his name with his bare hands, without any background or people to help, established the Ten Thousand People Clan, and elevated the clan to a member of the Five Sects.

After gently wiping Jang Ilso’s body, the servants began to dress him.

He wore a blood-red robe embroidered with a golden dragon and placed a gold crown on top of his head. Then, he wore jewelry on both wrists and fingers. Finally, after applying the red lip tint, the look of Jang Ilso, the lord whom Ho Ga-Myung was familiar with, was complete.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso, reclining on the bed in his fancy attire, looked at Ho Ga-Myung.

“So. What happened?”

“Mount Hua destroyed one pirate group.”

“Hahahah.”

Jang Ilso lightly covered his mouth with one hand and began to giggle.

“Those kids are so hot-tempered. I knew this would happen, but not this fast.”

“Thanks to that, the work is pushed forward. Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon destroyed the Great Whale Pirates and also eliminated the Black-Haired Ghost.”

“...so fast?”

“Something a little different than expected happened. It seemed like Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon didn’t bring the Mount Hua disciples but the Green Forest Bandits.”

“Oh my, just what does that guy eat?”

Jang Ilso, who guessed what was going on with just a few words, shook his head.

“It seems like he ate some poisonous snake; surely it has to be that. If we handle such a man wrongly, even we will get bitten. Hmm, fine, whatever. And the end?”

“Handled neatly?”

When Jang Ilso clicked his tongue and held out his hand, the waiting servant handed him a glass filled with alcohol. He clicked his tongue as if upset, shook his head, and sighed lightly.

“Hmm. A shame. Did you send enough money to the family?”

“We have enough for the family to live for the rest of their lives.”

“That’s a good thing.”

Jang Ilso, who took a sip, had his lips twitch.

“In reality, a person’s life is worth less than a penny. But since he gave up his life in exchange for a thousand coins, it wouldn’t be unfair, right?”

“Of course, clan leader.”

“If a rumor spreads of the family having money, flies might flock there, so send some kids to handle it.”

“I will.”

Ho Ga-Myung slightly bowed his head and answered.

This was Jang Ilso’s method.

He didn’t consider it a big deal to sacrifice someone for his own purposes. But the price was too high, monetarily. And when he thought he had finished calculating, he completely forgot that fact.

All thoughts of those who sacrificed their lives for his plan must have disappeared from Jang Ilso’s mind at that moment. Because he didn’t look back on the past.

“Faster than expected, but neater than expected. Nice. This is what Mount Hua is like. Hahaha!”

Jang Ilso, who was laughing as if it were fun, suddenly stood up straight.

“Ga-Myung!”

“Yes!”

“Send the message we prepared!”

“Will it be done right away? I think we should wait a little longer.”

“Yes, alcohol definitely gets more expensive as it ages. But sometimes, if you overcook it, it loses the fresh taste. There is a right time for everything.”

Jang Ilso slowly traced his lips with the tip of his finger.

“What little time there is will be filled by the time they arrive. Now is the time.”

“Yes.”

“Hahaha!”

Jang Ilso’s eyes shone brightly, like a lamp blown by the wind.

“I have waited for so long. For a long time... for this moment. I was so sick of waiting.”

The sound of him gritting his teeth could be heard from his red lips.

Nothing pained him more than waiting. But despite this, he was a person who could endure more deeply than anyone else in the world.

If he didn’t get what he wanted due to a moment’s impatience, the pain of waiting would become even more unbearable. Jang Ilso was a person who hated waiting, but he was also one who needed to get what he wanted.

And finally.

The time had come to end this long wait.

“Thanks to Mount Hua, the plan has been delayed by 10 years. How could I not be petty about it? That is how it should be. That way, I will feel rewarded for spitting out the bastard that got caught in my stomach and pulling him out! Hahahaha!”

His insane laughter echoed through the palace.

It was more bizarre and strange than the jingling sound from the jewelry he had on his body.

A faint smile appeared on Ho Ga-Myung’s lips as he saw this.

‘The world won’t even know.’

No, even if they did know, it didn’t matter.

Even a huge fire that burned the mountain for days or months started from a small ember. Just throwing that ember into the dry bushes could set the entire mountain on fire.

Once a fire started, the important thing was to put it out, not who started it.

It was time for the fire to catch soon.

The bush called Kanho was so dry that even a small spark could bring it to flame.

“We will implement the following plan.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes shone strangely.

“Ga-Myung, Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, clan leader.”

“What is Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon doing now?”

“... He is stuck on that island and cannot come out.”

“Keep an eye on him.”

“Uh?”

Ho Ga-Myung raised his head at the unexpected words and looked at Jang Ilso to discern his intentions.

“We don’t know what he might do. Don’t miss a thing because things might go wrong because of him.”

A strange light was shining in Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes.

‘Isn’t this the end of Mount Hua’s role?’

They had left them alone since they had a role to play, and now that their task was done, they had nothing more to do. Then why was Jang Ilso concerned about it?

A person who never looked back on the past.

‘Then....’

For Jang Ilso, Mount Hua was... no, did this mean that the role of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon didn’t end here?

“The answer?”

“... I will do so, clan leader.”

“Good. Then, shall we go see the flowers today?”

Jang Ilso got up from his seat and walked away humming. Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes, watching him, were dark.

‘I don’t know.’

He was too small to fully imagine the picture this giant would paint.

Chapter 779: Life Is Innately Painful (3)

“Kuakkkk!”

The dust-covered Taoist man moved his feet with difficulty and entered the cave.

“W-what is this place?”

Taoist.

Hyun Jong quickly looked around.

‘They have faced so many hardships!’

If only Chung Myung had arrived on time, there would have been no danger. But even so, the opponents were pirates. They weren’t people to be handled easily.

“Come quickly!”

“Yes! Sect leader!”

But now, they were no longer a minor concern.

Because they were the main force of Mount Hua, brought from Shaanxi, and had arrived with him at the Yangtze River.

Of course, the Mount Hua sect couldn’t deal with the Yangtze River pirates all alone, but they could take down one or two groups. Now, Mount Hua was no longer a weak, third-rate sect in Shaanxi!

An intense glow shone in Hyun Jong’s eyes as he saw the disciples standing firmly behind him.

“If you mess with my kids, you will have to pay! Let’s go!”

“Yes, sect leader!”

The disciples of the Mount Hua sect all had this scary atmosphere as they proudly moved through the place.

“... what did you say?”

“It has ended already, sect leader!”

“... already?”

“Yes. Everything has been wiped out.”

“...”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes, feeling dazed. The beggar who spoke looked at him with a slightly sad look.

“... could it be that the pirates were a small group?”

“Is that possible? It is one of the Eighteen Pirates’ places along the Yangtze River.”

“Right?”

“Among the pirates, there is a group called the Great Whale Pirates.”

“Great Star bandits?”

“The Great Whale Pirates! The Great Star bandits are in Green Forest!”

“... why are the bandits and pirates all named in the same manner? It isn’t even like they are making some stir-fried beef dish.”

“That’s what... isn’t that because it was made without much thought?”

“Who did?”

“Well. I don’t know.”

The beggar, frustrated trying to talk with Hyun Jong, who was completely lost, waved his hand.

“Well, the Great Whale pirates were destroyed. Not only the Great Whale Pirates but also one of the newly built pirate groups was completely annihilated. Right now, the entire river is a mess because of that.”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes again.

“... The entire river?”

“Yes.”

“Pirates?”

“Yes, right!”

“Can you explain what happened?”

“Ah. As for how that happened...!”

After a while, Hyun Jong, who had heard the entire story from the beggar, opened his mouth.

“... He brought the Green Forest with him?”

“Yes, right, sect leader.”

“... Green Forest?”

“Yes, that is correct.”

Hyun Jong’s mouth trembled as if it could fall off at any moment.

So... a Taoist brought bandits to defeat the pirates?

‘What does this mean?’

No, he understood it... considering that it was Chung Myung, it made sense...

As he was trying to force himself to understand, he heard whispering from behind.

“As expected, it is Chung Myung.”

“Actually, doesn’t it suit him better?”

“Better? No, they are perfect for each other!”

“Well, whether it is a Taoist or a bandit, we make a living in the mountains, so we are the same.”

Same? What was the same? You bastards!

Hyun Jong, who didn’t know where to go with these absurd events, felt like his stomach would explode. Strangely, a sense of pride welled up as he remembered what the first thing he had to ask was.

“So, where are the kids now?”

“Ah, that...”

A strange anxiety filled Hyun Jong’s face as he looked at the beggar’s expression.

“...”

Hyun Jong gazed at the island visible in the distance. Confusion filled his eyes.

“That... what are they doing?”

“Looks like they are building something.”

“No, it seems like there are a lot of ships crowded there.”

“Why are they there?”

“Well... at the very least, they are doing something useless again.”

At times like this, he really envied that mouth.

The Mount Hua disciples were not particularly surprised by the strange sight they were witnessing. They knew enough about Chung Myung to understand his peculiar ways.

‘Why can’t I still get used to this?’

At that moment, they saw a ship approaching from the other side.

“... it’s a ship.”

“No, maybe not. Can a normal ship move at that speed?”

Soon, a familiar face appeared at the bow of the rapidly approaching ship.

“Sect leader!”

“Uh... um, right, Baek Cheon.”

Baek Cheon flew from the ship and landed right in front of Hyun Jong, bowing respectfully. His face was full of joy.

“I greet the sect leader.”

“You have been through so much. Are you hurt?”

“Thanks to the sect leader’s concern and worry, we all came back safe.”

“Right. It is truly fortunate. Indeed... right, a relief...”

As Hyun Jong trailed off, Baek Cheon glanced at him. He knew what Hyun Jong was going to say, and everyone behind him knew it, too.

Sure enough, Hyun Jong asked while gesturing lightly.

“That... what is being done?”

“... that....”

Baek Cheon couldn’t quite explain it.

“Come and see for yourself....”

“... right. I guess we can do that.”

Hyun Jong didn’t have particularly high expectations. Although it was said that Baek Cheon or Yu Yiseol were the only ones who could stop Chung Myung from Mount Hua, they couldn’t literally stop him.

“Can we get on the ship?”

“Yes, Sect leader. First, there is a fixed number who can come on. So we will come again.”

Hyun Jong nodded weakly.

As the ship approached, several disciples, including Hyun Jong, climbed aboard without delay. Then, the ship turned and quickly headed to the island.

Although Hyun Jong didn’t have much experience with water, he could tell that the speed of the ship was unusual.

“... it doesn’t seem like there is any wind, so why is the ship moving so fast...”

“We have people rowing the ship below. Perhaps because they are all warriors, they are fast.”

In response to Baek Cheon’s explanation, Hyun Jong peeked over the railing and looked down. No matter how he examined the oars, which were rowing, they appeared to be wooden but felt like iron.

“... the people rowing are warriors?”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“...”

Hyun Jong couldn’t bear to ask why so many warriors were rowing under a ship. He could now roughly guess the reason.

As a trained warrior, his ears could also hear the groaning sounds coming from below.

“Move! Move quickly, you men! If you are late, you get beaten to death!”

“What are you, a ghost? Catch that bastard!”

“I don’t know what the ghost is doing, but I know that if we are late, they will catch and hit us! Shut up and move fast!”

“Dragon King! Please stop the bastard! Ugh!”

Hyun Jong shook his head and closed his eyes.

‘I didn’t hear a thing.’

It had to be some auditory hallucination.

Right, it has to be auditory hallucinations...

As Hyun Jong closed his eyes tightly, the image of the island began to appear in his mind.

No, it wasn’t the image of the island, but the image of ships anchored in a row in front of the island.

Such a strange sight.

When ships were anchored, they usually were moored around the island, but now the ships Hyun Jong was seeing were parked in a straight line from the island to the river.

That alone was odd, but all the ships were tied together with large chains, and large wooden boards were placed between them like bridges.

“T-that...”

At the end of the strange ship bridge, a group of people were clinging to each other like ants and shouting.

“Yah! I told you to hold on tight!”

“Make sure to clean it! Chain it up and put the wooden pegs in it!”

“If this thing shakes, our lives will shake too!”

All of them were shirtless men, holding on tightly as if trying to connect one ship to another. It looked like they were trying to secure the ship, but because of the strong water current, the ship rocked every time a wave hit.

“Ahhh! I am falling!”

“Ah!”

In the end, the space between the two ships, which had barely been kept together, widened, and those who were holding them in place fell into the river.

Plop! Plop! Plop!

“Ahh! Save me!”

“I am floating away!”

Those who fell into the water were being swept by the rapids, screaming and moving away. Still, those on the ship didn’t even look in that direction, as if it were a familiar occurrence.

“Narrow it down! Narrow it down! Pull the chain!”

“Ah, hold on tight! If we fall again this time, the boss said he’d throw us into the water!”

“Kuak! Hurry! Pull it quickly! Before my arms fall off!”

When the people who were holding the two ships with both hands tried hard, those holding the chains took advantage to narrow the space between the ships and somehow tie them together.

“That...”

Hyun Jong was about to say something but then went silent. It wasn’t because he had nothing to say or because he was flustered.

Something worse was happening in front of his eyes.

“Puaaak!”

“Eikkk!”

“I-I cannot breathe...”

Another group of people emerged from the river and began to pant as if they hadn’t breathed for years.

“I cannot do this... gurgle.”

“Yeah, come to your senses! If you go unconscious, you will drown here!”

“G-give me a plank! Please! I... I feel like I am going to die of the cold...”

“Mother...”

Neckless dogs.

Hyun Jong, who witnessed the terrible tragedy, closed his eyes in silence and then turned his head to look at Baek Cheon.

Baek Cheon had already turned his gaze elsewhere.

“... Baek Cheon.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“What about them?”

“They are pirates.”

“...”

Ah, they were the pirates.

Pirates had fallen into the water and were dying. Hahah....

“What are they doing now?”

“... two jobs.”

“Two?”

“Yes. One is to secure the anchor underwater. The current here is so strong that the ship cannot be anchored with an ordinary one....”

“...”

“So we are digging up the riverbed and planting specially made anchors. The anchor and chain are connected to secure the ship.”

“... and the other?”

“We are currently salvaging needed items from a sunken ship from the battle.”

“...”

Hyun Jong’s eyes turned to the pirates.

A mouse that fell into the water... no, calling them mice was a bit much, they were drowning dogs... no, this was also the same....

In any case, the faces of the pirates who had fallen into the water were now pale as water ghosts. Even they would never have imagined that they would end up like this in the water.

“... those pirates.... Are you making them work?“

“Yes. Every single one of them is precious labor....”

Right, that could be. That might happen in life. This would be more true if it was Chung Myung.

But, what he couldn’t understand at all wasn’t the bandits and the pirates who were captured by Taoists and forced to work, but the sparkling something that was connected from the island to their feet.

“Then, Baek Cheon.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“That person... why do they have something like that on their legs?”

“Ah, that...”

Baek Cheon lowered his gaze as if he couldn’t bear to say this while looking at the sect leader’s face.

“Chung Myung is saying that releasing the pirates into the water is the same as letting them run. They cannot be allowed to run away....”

“So you tied that to a person’s leg?”

“... yes.”

Hyun Jong didn’t ask any more questions and looked at the sky in silence.

The sky was very clear and blue today.

“Heheh... hahah... I sent you all to save people from turning into slaves, but you ended up using bandits and pirates as slaves. Haha... hahaha....”

Hyun Jong, who had been laughing as if he’d lost his mind, looked at Baek Cheon with a much fresher face. Baek Cheon was rather shocked. That relaxed face felt so scary.

“I know roughly what is happening.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“Let us go back to the main question.”

“... Yes.”

“What are you doing this for?”

This time, Baek Cheon looked up at the sky in silence.

Water filled those beautiful eyes.

Sect leader.

That... that was what he was also most curious about.

Chapter 780: Life Is Innately Painful (4)

“You have fun with your hands, you bandits!”

The roar of a lion echoed loudly.

The same scene Hyun Jong witnessed was unfolding on the other side of the island.

If there was any difference, it was the presence of Chung Myung.

One might think it was just a small difference, but the impact of that small difference was enormous.

“Euk...”

“That’s why I have no strength in my hands.”

Some of those who were whining and clinging to the ship slowly approached Chung Myung, who was standing on the shore.

“What?”

As Chung Myung rolled his eyes, the bandits all fell flat on the ground.

“Oh my, Taoist! If this continues, we’ll all die!”

“More than 30 people have fallen into the water and been washed away just this morning!”

“Everyone is so tired. Holding a ship that’s trying to drift away is harder than you can imagine.”

Chung Myung tilted his head as if he couldn’t understand the complaint.

“So?”

“... uh?”

“So?”

“...”

Language was communicated, but words weren’t always understood. Words only had meaning when both parties comprehended each other. Unfortunately, although someone was speaking, there was no one understanding.

The bandits, who were about to suggest giving up, changed their words when they saw Chung Myung’s genuinely puzzled expression.

“W-we need some rest....”

“What? Rest?”

“T-take a break...”

“Breakkk?”

Chung Myung’s eyes blazed at that.

“No, since you were stuck in the mountain playing the forest god, did you think you came down to play here too? Your limbs and legs are all still intact, and you want to rest in broad daylight!”

Actually... weren’t people usually called Taoists if they stayed up in the mountains and trained? If the Mount Hua Taoist was telling the bandits that they were just playing forest god....

And they worked all night too...

“Taoist, everyone is exhausted...”

“Exhausted?”

Chung Myung tilted his head slightly in disapproval.

“Those guys who can ride the mountain while roaring are now getting tired from just work? So this is what the Green Forest is! What? Would you like me to show you all what it means to be exhausted?”

“W-well, no....”

“Anyway, you people don’t have guts, guts! That’s why you all make a living by stealing! Come on, I will take this chance to completely change your mindset! Can’t you go back to your place and work right now?”

“Eikkk!”

The second elder, who was watching Chung Myung roll his eyes, ready to kick the bandits’ asses, opened his mouth to Im So-Byeong.

“Excuse me... Green Forest King.”

“Hmm?”

Im So-Byeong, who was sitting on the watchtower, admiring the scenery and drinking tea, looked at the second elder as if asking why he was being called.

“... you will leave that situation like that?”

“What about it?”

“I think it is a bit harsh...”

“Ah, those words he said now?”

Im So-Byeong, who elegantly put down the teacup on the table, smiled.

“He didn’t say anything wrong, right?”

“...”

“In the first place, the words were right. Now is the time to accept some harsh words.”

The second elder was silent at this, his nose turning cold.

It’s definitely a problem that a guy like that is called Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. It was clearly misfortunate to have Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon as the best warrior among the disciples, holding the position to be the best in the future, and being a full-on bastard.

But from the Green Forest’s point of view, the more problematic thing was that this person was the Green Forest King.

‘What will become of Kangho?’

A terrible situation was happening before him. The man who was at the peak of the Evil Factions, the guy who made a living out of banditry, was worrying about Kangho’s future.

At that time, a group of people jumped out of a ship that was approaching quickly through the currents.

“Taoist!”

“Hmm?”

As soon as they got off, they ran to Chung Myung with joyous looks.

“White Thunder cannons! All six have been recovered!”

“Woah! They really worked hard.”

“The pirates almost died. They managed to survive, but... no, it isn’t important. Anyway, we did as instructed here!”

“Oh?”

Chung Myung’s face cleared up and turned softer for the first time in a while.

“Found them all?”

“Yes, Taoist! Hehehe! Really... it was really so hard.... The chain was short, so we had to hold on to them and go into the water, so....”

“Yes, yes. You had a hard time.”

Chung Myung smiled happily as he listened to the words that briefly explained their hardships. Then suddenly, he asked.

“How many were there?”

“Six of them.”

“Oh?”

At that moment, Chung Myung launched himself like a thunderbolt and threw a flying kick at the bandit.

“Kuaak!”

With a scream like a pig getting slaughtered, the bandit fell back.

“You bastard! Do you think my eyes don’t work? What? How many? Six? I clearly saw the number of cannons on board the sunken ships when fighting! What? You say six? Sixxx?”

The body of the bandit lying on the ground trembled.

This poisonous bastard... in the midst of such a vicious fight, he was counting them?

“Ah, did I look like an ignorant bandit who couldn’t count? What! I will make sure to teach you how to count from now on!”

Chung Myung ran upright, got on top of the bandit, and began swinging his arms like windmills.

“One! Two! Five! Ten! Now count them, you bastard! Count how many times you get hit! If you get the last one wrong, you get hit right from the start again!”

“Ahhh! Ackkkk! Please spare me! Taoist! I did wrong! I made a bad mistake because it was so hard!”

“It was hard? It isn’t hard if you move, you bastard! Just die! Forget it!”

The faces of the bandits began to gradually turn gray.

In faraway places, thieves couldn’t move against the strong.

It wasn’t because they followed some rules, but because they did not follow worldly morals or laws in the first place, so they could not resist the oppression of those stronger using any means other than force.

What can they do?

Would they like to discuss morals with someone who spends his qi on the topic of bandits? Or would they rather just go to the office and report the pirates?

In that sense, Chung Myung was their natural enemy.

First, basically anyway... in his own way... since he was a member of the Justice faction, he had justification and was extremely powerful. A guy like that was pushing a bandit with scary eyes that made him confused.

“Just do it!”

Chung Myung took his eyes off the bandit lying down and looked around.

Everyone who made eye contact with him flinched and looked down.

“These thieves expect to be treated like human beings! Gather these people who are useless even if they survive, and throw them into the river bottom!”

The one who was working with the bandits was now criticizing them and losing his temper.

“Don’t even think about having a meal until you retrieve all the White Thunder cannons!”

“T-then, what do we eat?”

“Just catch a fish and eat it! It is meat all over!”

“...”

“Oh, and don’t even think about sleeping until the ships are all set!”

“...”

“Now go straighten your back and do the work right! Will you not work? It is even harder if you take breaks between work! Finish it quickly and take a break! I will definitely make you all do it! If you always have that mindset, there is nothing a person cannot do!”

The nagging made their ears bleed.

‘I want to go back to the mountains.’

‘It would have been better to get scolded by the leader.’

But unfortunately, this was an island.

There was no way for the bandits to escape from there under the watchful eyes of Chung Myung and Im So-Byeong. All of them had iron chains on their legs. No matter how skilled one was, wasn’t it just a dream to run from Chung Myung while wearing these iron chains?

‘This is slavery. This is slavery.’

‘Ugh, my life...’

The faces of the bandits turned pale and lifeless.

In the first place, Chung Myung didn’t particularly show mercy to normal people. Still, he was a person who didn’t even understand why bandits needed to be treated like normal people.

Chung Myung, himself a bandit by nature, lived with patience (?) according to the teachings of Mount Hua. So, on what basis could he understand people who lived their lives by looting others?

“Well, these bastards need to get beaten more to come to their senses!”

“Chung Myung.”

“What! Bastard...”

Uh?

Instead of cursing, Chung Myung slowly turned around.

“What?”

Why was this face here?

He tilted his head, and his expression, despite being awkward, softened a bit.

“Uh... When did you come, sect leader?”

“... I just arrived.”

“Ah... if you came, you could have contacted....”

No, they could have contacted him too.

If they hadn’t contacted him, then how could they have known the place....

Chung Myung slowly tilted his head to the side and saw Baek Cheon hiding behind Hyun Jong.

“...your sasuk did....”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung mumbled through gritted teeth, then his expression completely changed, and he smiled brightly at Hyun Jong.

“Sect leader, you had a hard time coming this far, right? Welcome to... ah! Ack! Ear! My ear! Ahhh! My ear, sect leader! My ear will fall off!”

“Come here.”

“Ear! Let go of my ear! Ahh!”

Despite Chung Myung’s harsh words, Hyun Jong smiled, pulling the ear further.

“I ordered you to stop civilians from being sold as slaves, and now you are directly using pirates and bandits as slaves? Let’s go, and you will listen carefully and then tell me what the hell is happening.”

The tyrant was dragged along, leaving footprints in the sand. In the meantime, he glared at Baek Cheon with angry eyes, but Baek Cheon just slightly turned his head and looked away.

“Ackkk! Baek Cheonnn! Jin Dong-Ryonggggg! There was no need to bring an adult into the matter without speaking... Ahhh! Ear! Sect leader! Sect leader! My ear! Ahhh... it is being torn... no, I mean it!”

“Shut your mouth and follow me! I hate myself already!”

“Sect leader!”

The bandits all looked like they had seen a ghost at the sight of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon being dragged around.

“... did Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon get dragged out?”

“By the ears?”

Those present clearly saw with their own eyes how Chung Myung had taken down two pirate troops, ran on water, and cut the ships with his sword.

Seeing such a terrifying human being dragged away, screaming like this, felt absurd to them.

“... that person is the sect leader of Mount Hua....”

“Oh my... he is so strong that he could easily subdue a monster like him.”

“Hmmm.”

Baek Cheon cleared his throat very slightly.

It seemed like an unnecessary misunderstanding was happening... well, he didn’t think he had to correct anything.

“That guy will not be able to come out for a while, so everyone, take some time to rest.”

“T-thank you, Taoist!”

“Ahem... we can finally rest!”

“Sect leader! Hurray!”

The bandits of the Evil Faction, who were supposed to be the opposite of the Taoist factions of the Justice faction, began to praise the sect leader of Mount Hua in unison.

If you look at that without knowing the meaning, one might think this was a historic sight of murim harmony, but the inside was salty and tearful.

“But why did he come all the way here?”

“Well... from the looks of it, it seems like he brought a bunch of people from Mount Hua with him.”

“Could it be that he predicted the situation in advance and came to occupy this place?”

“Eh. No... that person isn’t some saint.”

“No, no. If you think about it, it is true. Even though Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon is the strongest, isn’t he still one of the Third Class disciples? It doesn’t make sense for the Third Great disciple to make a decision to occupy an island and create his own base.”

“Uh? I guess that’s what I heard?”

The bandits looked at one another.

“The Mount Hua’s sect leader predicted....”

“As expected, I heard that Taoists with deep understanding read the secrets of Heaven. It is wonderful.”

“... Should I also step into Taoism?”

“Heavenly Buddha!”

“Immense Peaceful Buddha!”

Baek Cheon closed his eyes tightly.

‘I am sorry.’

He didn’t have the courage or confidence to correct it.

What... and actually, no matter what the intention was, the result was good....

“Heavenly Buddha....”

A weak chant flowed from Baek Cheon’s mouth.

Chapter 781: Let's Go Die Somewhere And Have Fun (1)

“... And so that happened!”

“....”

After hearing the entire explanation, Hyun Jong stared at Chung Myung with an expression that was difficult to explain.

“And therefore...”

His eyes scanned the people sitting in front of him.

Chung Myung, Baek Cheon, Baek Sang, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, Jo Gul, Tang Soso, and even Hae Yeon with Im So-Byeong.

“Occupy the land.”

“Yes!”

“Running a pirate group.... is the plan?”

“Oh my, sect leader! I explained it to you in a good way, but you still don’t understand!”

Chung Myung pounded his chest in frustration.

“It isn’t about stealing, it is about collecting a fair toll, right?”

“... you collect the toll on the river?”

“Yes!”

“Don’t pirates do that?”

“....”

“...”

Chung Myung and Hyun Jong looked at one another.

Of course, in the complex intersection of gazes, it was Chung Myung who turned his gaze first.

“No, well... if you say that, it might be true depending on what you think....”

Shhh.

“Eik!”

“Now, why are you pulling out the sword?”

“Wait! Just calm down first!”

“Let go! Aren’t you going to let me go!?”

Hyun Jong removed the disciples who were holding onto him. His eyes were red.

“Now that I am even hearing that Mount Hua disciples are going to try and steal! I realize that I should have cut that guy down before he did this!”

“Do you have the skills to do that?”

“Hyun Young, whose side are you on, you bastard!”

“What side, you ask? Of course, I am on Chung Myung’s side.”

Bubbles frothed from Hyun Jong’s mouth.

He chose the wrong person to ask. Yes, Hyun Young was definitely on Chung Myung’s side.

At that time, Hyun Jong spoke in a slightly calmer voice.

“And depending on how you think about it, it isn’t that wrong.”

“Don’t you think charging a toll is wrong?”

“It makes no sense to say that we are pirates just because we collect taxes. Then, are all the people on a ship who take money for putting people on a ship pirates?”

“....”

“If you think about it that way, everyone who drives a ship on the Yangtze River is a pirate. Is the Yangtze River such a place?”

“.... You too.”

Hyun Jong sat down. After listening to all this, it didn’t seem so wrong.

“... No, I said the same thing...”

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Words inherently have different meanings and conveyance depending on who says them.”

“...”

Chung Myung also thought that way and knew that what he said wasn’t wrong, but he felt strangely bad.

Hyun Young glanced at the sullen face, smiled, and spoke again to Hyun Jong.

“If you think about it, it isn’t a bad idea. If the term toll tax bothers you, you can just collect the fare when you take people on a ship going to and from the island. Let’s just leave the island for free.”

“Umm....”

“More than anything, isn’t it true that there is no guarantee that something like this won’t happen again in the future? It isn’t possible to chase him this far and catch him every time. Mount Hua should also provide a safe route for the Eunha merchants. The losses are so great now.”

“Okay. That is true....”

Hyun Jong thought for a moment, then nodded.

In the past, he might have quit the talks, but now he understood a bit about how businesses run. Thanks to that, he knew how important this work was.

“But we will be occupying this place? What about training? What about our actual mountain?”

“Ah, that is right.”

“Uh?”

“The Green Forest will handle this. I am done talking with the Green Forest King.”

Hyun Jong blinked his eyes and then looked away. Im So-Byeong had a smile as he nodded.

“Yes.”

“... You mean, Green Forest?”

“Yes.”

“The Yangtze River?”

“I didn’t become a bandit because I liked the mountains. One turns to banditry because they need to make a living. What does it matter if it is the mountain or water? As long as we get money?”

That was right... well, it was true. Besides, whether they were bandits or pirates, they were all the same.

“I know what the sect leader is worried about. But now, Green Forest is also following the will of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon and the Heavenly Friends Alliance. We are increasing our business while not harming civilians and only charging appropriate toll fees.”

“... isn’t collecting the toll a problem in itself?”

“We provide an escort instead. We escort travelers until they cross the mountain. Those who were previously victims of looting and thieves here would welcome it with open arms.”

Welcome them with open arms?

Bandits?

“That... the bandits... no, does Green Forest do that?”

“There are some people who feel irritated and stifled by small things. It isn’t like we can help them all. If you don’t like something, you don’t do it. Simple.”

“... it doesn’t have to be a ‘not do it’ just because I hate it.”

Everyone turned to look at Hae Yeon at the sound of his voice. Hae Yeon’s face, which had been calm, suddenly turned bright red.

“I don’t like it.”

“It seems like he really doesn’t like it.”

“For some reason, even if you die in Shaolin, nothing good will happen.”

“A-amitabha! Buddha! Amitabha!”

“Just say infinite Buddha.”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue and opened his mouth again.

“Anyway, it isn’t bad. It isn’t like we are messing around, and Green Forest will handle things. In return, we can safely ensure the Eunha merchants pass and collect half the toll fees from merchants passing by.”

Oh? Escort?

That was a fair distribution of profits.

If he thought about it, Mount Hua wouldn’t have to do anything... yes, right...

At that time, Hyun Sang, who was silently observing the situation, opened his mouth.

“Chung Myung.”

“Yes.”

“I understand what you were thinking when you did this. But I am anxious. This is the Yangtze River. You have already ruined two pirate groups, so if you occupy this place and sit here, will the other pirates stay still?”

“Ah, that?”

“On the way, I saw you setting up cannons around the island and a new bridge. But the Yangtze River path isn’t an easy way to block with just a few cannons. You are not looking down on the water pirates just because you took down one or two, right?”

“Eh. As if?”

Chung Myung waved his hand dismissively.

If the Black Dragon King were to come with all his troops, this island would fall in less than half a day. To prevent that, a large troop, whether Green Forest or Mount Hua, would have to be stationed here.

But that was impossible.

“You don’t have to worry about that.”

“Uh?”

“The Black Dragon King will not be able to attack this place.”

“...Why?”

“Maybe soon, this won’t be such a big deal.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung’s lips twitched.

“In the past, they would have attacked right away, but the Black Dragon King cannot move like that now.”

“Why? What is happening with the Black Dragon King?”

“No, it isn’t that something is happening; it’s that something is going to happen now.”

“...I don’t have a clue what you mean.”

“You will soon find out.”

Chung Myung curled his lips into a smile.

“If I am right, things will start happening right now. So just wait a little while.”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung with an expression of confusion.

“Did you do something?”

“It is true that I did something. But it will happen even if I don’t because there are people other than me who can take care of it.”

“Just what?”

“I don’t know for sure about that, but...”

Chung Myung thought for a moment and then smiled.

“One couldn’t refuse when the plan is laid out for them, right? Let’s have fun in that case.”

Unlike his brightly smiling mouth, his eyes were dark.

On the surface, there was nothing particularly conspiratorial about the situation. But Chung Myung’s intuition never missed the strange things that flowed between happenings.

Usually, when one gets this feeling on the battlefield, there would be a well-laid trap in front of you.

In such a case, there were just three ways to escape the trap.

One, do not go.

The other was to break through with skills.

And finally...

‘Make matters bigger than the other person can handle as a trap.’

If you created a pirate, you could act like one.

Instead, he would reap the benefits. Not money or anything like that, but real benefits.

Chung Myung’s gaze went beyond Hyun Jong and looked somewhere far away. All one could guess was that he was looking at a very distant place, but no one could tell what kind of scene he was envisioning.

Hyun Jong sighed softly.

‘I don’t know.’

He knew that the kid’s thoughts were beyond his understanding, so he had no choice but to trust him. But Hyun Jong was worried that this would place another burden on Chung Myung.

“Right. First, I know what you think. Then, can we just leave this matter to Green Forest and return to Mount Hua?”

“Ah, yes. We can, but...”

“Uh?”

“I was waiting for you to come. You brought a lot of disciples with you, right?”

“I... did?”

Chung Myung clapped loudly and laughed like a kid.

“Then let’s get started right away. We have one simple thing to do.”

“One simple thing?”

For some reason, those words made Hyun Jong feel even more uneasy.

A large ship came into the dock.

Soon, a large rope was thrown from the ship, firmly connecting the ship to the land. A large wooden plank stretched out between the ship and the land, and people began to get off the ship one by one.

For some reason, the faces of everyone who got off the ships looked like they had narrowly escaped death.

“Damn it!”

One of the merchants who disembarked was so furious that he threw his luggage to the floor.

Normally, it would have been packed with valuables and handled with care. Still, the man hurled it to the ground without the slightest hesitation, letting out an angry roar.

“Again! Again! Shit!”

Then, the other merchants waiting to board the ship approached, their faces filled with concern.

“...Did you get robbed again?”

“Damn it! Everything was taken except for the bag! This... damn it, if this keeps happening, how are we supposed to make a living!”

“Which pirates?”

“I don’t know. These people don’t even reveal who they are anymore! They just approach on small ships and steal everything that can make money!”

“Euk.”

“I am going insane.”

The merchants’ faces were filled with sorrow.

It might be surprising that the Yangtze River was experiencing such turmoil, but the recent behavior had been unusual.

In the past, if they paid the toll, their goods wouldn’t be taken. But now, these nameless thieves were stealing everything.

As this happened constantly, merchants became afraid to board the ships traveling on the Yangtze River.

“How many times has this happened!”

“What are the officials doing! Why are they leaving the pirates alone!”

“Have the officials ever done anything properly? At the very least, they could be taking money from the pirates and making a living too!”

“Shh! Lower your voice, man!”

“Did I say something wrong?”

Excited people had no control over what they could or couldn’t say.

“Damn it, I am trying to make a living, but if they keep getting ripped off like this, how will I be able to feed my wife and kids? It would be better to get slashed by a sword and die! The entire river is full of pirates! Be it the Nine Great Sects or the officials, no one is interested.”

“Huh. Not everyone is uninterested.”

“What?”

“Didn’t you hear? Mount Hua!”

“Mount Hua?”

“I heard that the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s Mount Hua destroyed the Great Whale pirates. And in the meantime, didn’t they say that they also subjugated a newly formed pirate troop?”

“M-Mount Hua? Why Mount Hua?”

“Is there a reason for Mount Hua to do that? Isn’t Mount Hua the place where the bandits who went on a rampage were attacked, broke into the mountains, destroyed the bandits’ hideouts, robbed their storehouses, and helped people with that?”

“... they did?”

“This time, after hearing that the pirates had captured civilians, I was full of courage and came running here.”

“Huh... oh my... is that true?”

“Eh! It is so late to hear news from a merchant, you know Mister Cheon, right?”

“I know.”

“Didn’t they say that he was kidnapped by a group of bandits and was rescued by Mount Hua’s disciples? That guy told that story 15 times a day. It will stick to your ears anymore.”

“Mister Cheon isn’t the type of person to make up things that didn’t exist.”

“Right. The Nine Sect bastards are just watching and not doing anything, but this isn’t the case with the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Oh my, there is no such document....”

“Besides, it seems like Mount Hua’s sect leader made a decision this time.”

“A decision?”

“They say they will occupy a place on the Yangtze River and launch ships so that merchants and those who want to cross the river can do so without a problem.”

“I-is that true?”

“Right. Besides, it isn’t that far from here. So even if you can’t go by river, crossing it won’t be a problem, right?”

“Just that alone takes the breath away! But would the pirates just sit and watch it?”

“What will they do, not sitting still? The Heavenly Friends Alliance is a place where Sichuan Tang and the Palaces side with Mount Hua! I’m saying it isn’t a place where people like pirates could do anything.”

“... After hearing it, maybe it is right.”

Those who were talking once again realized the power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

From their perspective, the most feared and grim reaper-like threat was the pirates. Since it was a place that even the famous Nine Great Sects couldn’t touch, there was no way to break through, so they couldn’t help but be more afraid.

However, they were unable to do anything about Mount Hua, who invaded their waters and set up camp.

“Was the power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance that strong?”

“I plan to use this chance to change business partners as well.”

“Business partners?”

“Think about it. If the Yangtze River is blocked like this, isn’t it natural for the merchants to suffer damage? However, if Mount Hua goes out and creates a road, merchants with connections to the Heavenly Friends Alliance will be able to pass by the road without any problems.”

“... Yes.”

“Then which will do better in the future? It is obvious.”

“Eh... but still, the Nine Sects are....”

“Tsk, tsk. A person called a merchant! If you wait like that, the Heavenly Friends Alliance will surpass the Nine Sects, and you won’t be left with anything to work with! One can make a lot of money by looking at the future and making moves in advance.”

“...”

“Anyway, I am going to do it so you can hurry and find your way. I heard that within 10 days, Mount Hua will have an entire pathway set up.”

Each of the merchants thought and blinked.

‘Is that real?’

‘If this is true...’

Some were just surprised, while others were amazed.

However, a few astute and keen individuals realized that the world was changing.

Mount Hua from Shaanxi, or rather the Heavenly Friend Alliance to which it belonged, had now begun to extend its influence all the way to the Yangtze River. It might be a small change now, but it was difficult to imagine how significant this impact would be in the future.

“I hope things go well for Mount Hua.”

“Why?”

“Whether they came looking for profit or following an agreement, isn’t Mount Hua the only one who helped us when we were having a tough time? Those damned old sects just talk and negotiate but never actually do anything!”

“Right!”

“Right, you damn bastards!”

“Destroy everything!”

Among those who threw their lives into the Yangtze River, admiration for the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua, along with disappointment in the Nine Great Sects, gradually spread and grew in size.

Rumors about this situation also spread quickly and soon reached Shaolin and Wudang.

The scale of what started as a small subjugation grew at a fast rate.

Like... like an ember burning in a dry forest.

Chapter 782: Let's Go Die Somewhere And Have Fun (2)

“Mount Hua...”

The face of Venerable Heo Do, Wudang’s sect leader, went cold as if covered in thin ice.

After finishing the report, Heo Sanja gulped from the cold qi emanating from him.

Venerable Heo Do held the table, refusing to let go of his anger.

Even after suffering a crushing defeat in the last spar, he hadn’t changed much from the past. He just shook his head, thinking he needed to make up for what was lacking.

But it seemed he couldn’t maintain his composure over just one word.

“They don’t sit still even for a moment... Mount Hua!”

Venerable Heo Do, who spat out the words, glared at Heo Sanja.

“They subdued the pirates?”

“Yes. Thanks to that, the residents around the Yangtze River praise Mount Hua....”

“They are probably cursing us.”

“... This is also due to Mount Hua. Maybe because we are busy. But the pirates have been running rampant, and that is unusual.”

“I guess the reason is good. What matters is the result. As a result, Mount Hua is being praised again, and we are being criticized, right?”

“... Uh?”

Venerable Heo Do, who was silent for a moment, opened his mouth.

“Prepare the disciples.”

“Uh?”

“I will go personally. I need to subdue at least one pirate. A couple if I can.”

Heo Sanja was shocked and shouted.

“Now, sect leader. Are you saying that you will head to the Yangtze River?”

“Yes.”

“Why... sect leader. Those who are talking now will soon forget this anyway. Is there any need to waste energy on it?”

“People won’t regret it.”

“... Uh?”

“But there is Mount Hua there. Have you forgotten about it? Mount Hua knows how to take the fame which has soared in such a period and use it to their advantage.”

“...”

“If we leave them alone this time, they will do something more ridiculous again and try to get their hands on the Yangtze River.”

“Sigh, do you...”

Bang!

Venerable Heo Do hit the table.

“Isn’t it even more foolish to ignore Mount Hua after being humiliated like this?”

“...”

“If it were the former Mount Hua, I wouldn’t have cared much. But it is no longer the Mount Hua we used to know. It isn’t just that they have gotten stronger. The problem is that Mount Hua created a force called the Heavenly Friend’s Alliance.”

“...”

“There is no fool who would choose Mount Hua over the Nine Sects. But if it is the Heavenly Friends Alliance instead of the Nine Sects, it would be a different story! If you give them an inch at a time like this, the positions will eventually reverse.”

“How can they be compared to the Nine Great Sects?”

“It is still too early. Not yet.”

“...”

“But the first time I heard the name of Mount Hua, I said the same thing.”

Heo Sanja closed his mouth.

“But what about now? Mount Hua, which was once laughable, is now stabbing us with their sharp daggers. Is it important to save face even when things end up like this?”

“...”

“Stop talking nonsense and get ready. I will go to the Yangtze River. One pirate group has been taken, but it isn’t too late now.”

Even Heo Sanja knew that there was nothing wrong with what the sect leader said. To begin with, the sect leader’s thoughts were different in depth. Of course, they would look further and deeper.

But even so, Heo Sanja couldn’t shake off the hesitation.

This was because it wasn’t easy to determine whether Venerable Heo Do’s command came from deep insight or from ill feelings towards Mount Hua.

“Sect leader. I...”

It was then.

“Sect leader!”

A loud voice was heard outside the door.

“We are discussing something important, come later.”

“Sect leader... well, a letter arrived.”

“I will see it later.”

“This is a letter from the Shaolin abbot.”

“...bring it in.”

“Yes.”

When the door opened, the person who came in handed a letter and went out again. After opening the envelope and unfolding the letter, the lips of the sect leader’s mouth twitched strangely a moment later.

Heo Sanja, who was examining him, cautiously asked.

“May I ask about what it is?”

“They are sending disciples from Shaolin to the Yangtze River.”

“... Uh?”

“They say they want to subdue the pirates. He pretends to be all polite, but it seems like he couldn’t hold back this time.”

Heo Sanja’s eyes widened.

‘So, even Shaolin?’

Then, it meant that his sect leader’s thoughts were the same as Shaolin.

This meant 2 things. The judgment of Venerable Heo Do wasn’t wrong. And...

‘Does it mean that Mount Hua has now become a sect capable of influencing both Wudang and Shaolin, who can be the leaders of Nine Great Sects?’

A sense of change is probably a word that should be used in times as such.

“If we keep wasting time, Shaolin will also take the advantage. Get ready. I will personally lead the disciples to the Yangtze River.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

Now that things were like this, Heo Sanja couldn’t stop Venerable Heo Do anymore.

“I will prepare them right away.”

As Heo Sanja quickly went out, Venerable Heo Do, who was left alone in the room, looked at the table with sunken eyes.

‘Mount Hua...’

He would not let them run amok any longer.

No more.

The whole world was realizing the changed status of Mount Hua.

“You need to know who to thank. This is all thanks to Mount Hua, so be thankful to them.”

“What do you mean? Is this all because of Mount Hua? Of course, it is true that Mount Hua started the subjugation, but we couldn’t say that all of it was thanks to them, right?”

“Don’t speak nonsense! If Mount Hua hadn’t come out, the Nine Sects or Five Families wouldn’t have even come close to the Yangtze River. Wasn’t it Mount Hua, the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, who came to subdue the pirates of the Yangtze River and win our support? The others got scared of the support they would get and rushed to the Yangtze River.”

“... that is a bit of a twisted idea.”

“Then are you saying those people didn’t touch the Yangtze River alone for decades because they didn’t know about the existence of the pirates?”

“...”

“Stop being naïve! That’s exactly what those people aim for. We should thank Mount Hua! Didn’t they come to the Yangtze River without any need and subdue the pirates?”

“Right. I am grateful to Mount Hua.”

“See. In my opinion, it won’t be long until the Heavenly Friends Alliance, to which Mount Hua belongs, can defeat the Nine Great Sects.”

“Now, now, being delusional again.”

“I am being real!?”

Some people were in favor of Mount Hua, while others defended the Nine Great Sects, which had a deep-rooted history.

What was certain was that no matter which side they favored, the scale of fighting on the Yangtze River was growing.

And...

The results of these sporadic battles began to take a course that no one in the world expected.

A violent torrent that could completely change the course of the river, and the stage was spreading across the world along the Yangtze River.

Chapter 785: Let's Go Die Somewhere And Have Fun (5)

Nanjing.

“Damn, it isn’t sticking!”

Go Tae threw the card in his hand onto the board and scratched his head with an annoyed look on his face.

“Hehehe. There are such days.”

“What? Such days? It would be faster to find a day when it wasn’t like this!”

He quickly got up and left the room in a huff.

“It is sunny today.”

“It is just evening, and you are already trying to leave?”

“The more you try, the more you lose. Why do it? You want to steal a drink?”

“This bastard. Didn’t you do it last time?”

“How much does it cost!”

He glared harshly at the person sitting across from him and then kicked the chair in anger.

“Whatever, I am going to leave, so you guys play well among yourselves.”

“Where are you going? Did you forget you have work today?”

“How many people are supposed to stay in this small gambling house? I will collect the money and come back, so you people can continue.”

“Now, now, you have a bad temper, tsk.”

Regardless of whether someone was watching him or not, Go Tae picked up the sword he had left lying around and walked out of the gambling house at a rough pace.

As he walked along the back alley for a while, he saw merchants engaged in talks.

“The fruit is deliciously ripe! Try it!”

“But this! This is nice!”

“This is fabric which came from Xi’an! Take a look and go!”

Go Tae, who had been closely looking at that, frowned and strode to one side.

“Hey, elder!”

“Uh? Come and... uh....”

The merchant who discovered Go Tae instantly contorted his face. But for a moment, the merchant, who had a calm face, asked with an awkward look.

“Oh, you are here?”

“So? How is it? Is the business happening well?”

“Haha... ha. It is, as always, in business. But thanks to your concern, I am able to keep my mouth full.”

“Is that so?”

Go Tae chuckled.

“The elder puts treats right in my mouth, but I am unable to even put tree bark in my mouth.”

“Why...”

“What do you mean why?”

Kwang!

He mercilessly kicked away the items the old man was selling.

“Oh my!”

“If you made money, shouldn’t you have paid the rent? If you make money and throw it into your own old mouth, is that the end? You are already three months behind on paying me!”

“Oh my, please... I really don’t have any money.”

“You don’t have money? Then close the damn business! What are you doing in a business when you don’t even make enough to pay rent? Just go up a mountain and cut down a tree!”

“If you could just wait another month....”

“But you know, old man, do you think someone makes a business by digging in the ground?”

Bang!

He kicked the cart loaded with goods and threw it away, yelling.

“I was generous enough to wait for two more months? And what? Another month? Is this old man insane, or what? Want to die?! Do you think our Ghost Scorpion Clan is funny?”

“N-No! Why would I?”

“If you cannot pay money, then you should pay with something!”

Go Tae walked ahead with long strides and grabbed the cart he had kicked. The merchant, scared, grabbed the hem of Go Tae’s clothes.

“I-If you take these away, my family will starve to death! I will definitely give it next month, so just this once...!”

“Is this old man insane!”

Puak!

Go Tae grabbed the merchant’s leg and kicked him in the chest. The merchant screamed helplessly and fell down. Go Tae shook off the clothes the merchant had grabbed and spoke loudly.

“This is why one shouldn’t help these black-haired things. When this old man first started the business, he said he would pay as much rent as I wanted, and now he is making me a bad person? You could have just waited for two months, then! My baby ended up starving because you kept trying to feed your baby!”

Other merchants glanced at the scene from afar and whispered.

“What happened again?”

“I don’t know. Looks like that insane guy lost money at the gambling house again. This doesn’t happen just once or twice.”

“... how long do we have to watch this....”

Complaints and sighs came from everyone’s mouths. But no one dared to step forward and stop Go Tae.

For them, who were just normal people, dealing with Go Tae, a member of an Evil Faction sect, was tantamount to suicide. Just looking at the blade hanging and shaking on his side gave them chills.

As the old merchant was kicked away again, the other merchants couldn’t bear to watch and closed their eyes.

“If you are useless, you need to get hit.”

“Don’t say that. If I quit the business, what will I do for a living?”

“Would you not be able to make a living if you cultivated a crop?”

“What crop? I heard there are bandits in the mountains! There is no place to live without being robbed!”

“... damn it.”

At that moment, Go Tae grabbed the cart with one hand and looked around.

“Where is the rat squeaking around?”

Seeing that blood-thirsty gaze, the merchants quickly closed their mouths and lowered their eyes. There was a moment of silence.

“Starting from this month, those who cannot pay their rent on time will be kicked out, so keep that in mind! Are these shameless people trying to eat up everything they can and not even pay back?”

“W-would I do that?”

“And you should know that starting next month, the rent will be raised by a percentage.”

“Ah, no! Man! If you suddenly say that out of the blue...”

“It was decided by the leader, not me. It is no use whining, so get your money ready in time!”

Go Tae, with fear in his eyes, turned around and dragged the cart. No, he tried to hide.

“Y-You cannot go!”

The merchant whose cart had been taken away was holding onto the pant leg of Go Tae, blood dripping from his mouth.

“Does this old man want to die?”

“If you take that, I will die. Whether I die this way or that way, it is the same then! So please... please...”

“Oh, my? Is it the same?”

Go Tae put down the cart and took out the blade.

The merchants who heard the pulling sound were frightened and stepped back, their faces full of anxiety and confusion.

“S-someone, please stop him!”

“No! You cannot do that!”

“Gold man!”

The people around him screamed, but the merchant holding Go Tae seemed to have no intention of backing down. Instead, he was so angry that he started cursing.

“Right! Better I die! You thief! No matter how much money I earn doing business, it gets taken away! I don’t want to live like that either! Better to just kill me!”

“Haha, see this man?”

Puak!

Go Tae kicked the merchant in the chest and grabbed the blade tightly.

“Okay. If you want me to kill you, I will kill you! Today is your final day!”

It was the moment when he was shouting angrily and swinging the sword.

“Stop!”

“...!”

Go Tae slowly turned his head at the sound of a voice coming from somewhere.

If the merchants around him had shouted, they probably wouldn’t have even heard him, but the voice heard now was filled with qi.

Sure enough, people wearing blue uniforms were approaching.

“Spit!”

Go Tae glared at those approaching with unhappy eyes and spat on the floor.

“Oh my, did the people from the Chasing Righteousness clan come all the way here?”

“Go away!”

“Go away?”

A clear sneer appeared on the lips of Go Tae.

“Your side is the one to step down. Have you forgotten that this is our land? I don’t know since when the Chasing Righteousness clan has started to set foot in our area.”

The two of them glared at Go Tae.

“Area?”

“Right, area. This is our area. Did you forget that we decided not to approach one another? Or not? What? You want to start a war?”

Instead of responding to those words, the middle-aged man stared at Go Tae with a strange expression. Go Tae looked more triumphant as he spoke.

“Why? It seems like your spine has become numb to hear that even the world’s Nanjing Sword would be dragged into war, isn’t it?”

“War...”

A twisted smile appeared on the lips of Nanjing’s Sword, Cho Sang-Gil.

“There’s nothing we cannot do.”

“What?”

Go Tae was taken aback by those words.

‘Is this guy insane for real?’

The Chasing Righteousness clan, to which Nanjing’s Sword belonged, and the Ghost Scorpion clan, to which Go Tae belonged, had signed a treaty to not encroach on each other’s land. Therefore, they interacted with each other like cows and chickens over the border of Nanjing.

But today, that man suddenly led his disciples and invaded the territory of the Ghost Scorpion clan.

“What is happening?”

“Those bastards! Who do you think I am?”

As if they had heard the news that the Chasing Righteousness clan had appeared, the Ghost Scorpion clan members inside the gambling hall came rushing out. And behind the disciples of the Chasing Righteousness clan, people were being added one by one.

The two sides were engulfed in tension. Dozens of disciples from each clan gathered together, glaring at each other with angry eyes.

“Looks like Nanjing’s Sword’s soul will be spilled from his stomach.”

“You are the ones who went overboard first.”

“What?”

Cho Sang-Gil twitched his lips and sneered.

“The reason we have been letting your wicked behavior go unnoticed all this time is because the 18 River Families were behind you. You probably didn’t think I’d put up with it until now just because I was afraid of you people, right?”

“This bastard...”

“But that ends now.”

“... What is that supposed to mean?”

“Wudang and Shaolin are subjugating the pirate troops. That means the fate of the pirates is now sealed.”

“...”

“Then there is no reason why we should just sit by and watch little jerks like you act out. Disgusting rats. From today, there will be no place for you to step into Nanjing! I will give you one chance. Pack your bags and get out of Nanjing right now. If not, you will all die today.”

Go Tae’s face contorted with embarrassment. This was an unexpected situation.

‘A-are these people insane?’

Were they really going to war now?

This wasn’t something he could answer. This had to be a decision of his superiors...

“Three.”

“W-wait! W-wait! Cho Sang-gil!”

“Two.”

“N-Now!”

“One. Hit! Today we will drive out the Ghost Scorpion Sect from Nanjing!”

“Yes!”

Nanjing’s Sword gave an order without giving him time to reply. The waiting disciples drew their swords and rushed at once.

“Defeat them all!”

“You bastards! Do you think we have all been patient with you all this time because we had no strength?”

As the Chasing Righteousness Clan members rushed in, the Ghost Scorpion clan also drew their weapons.

“What are you doing! Knock down those novice ascetics and kill them!”

“Are you saying these people are looking down on us!”

“Kill them all!”

As the swords and blades clashed in the middle of the cast, the surrounding area instantly turned into a mess, with blood spurting in all directions.

“O-Oh my! What is happening!”

The merchants who were there all hurriedly ran or moved back.

The two clans paid no heed to that and continued with their bloody fight.

‘Oh god, this is war.’

‘Nothing like this has happened in 10 years.’

‘The Chasing Righteousness Clan has finally drawn its sword!’

At that moment, a beggar crouching in the corner of the square quietly stood up with a serious expression.

“... a war, huh.”

This wasn’t a simple fight. They were fighting for each other’s survival. No matter who won, the landscape of Nanjing would change.

‘We need to inform the superiors of this.’

He started running towards the hut in a huff.

Nanjing, which had maintained some semblance of peace on the outside, was suddenly engulfed in chaos.

And it wasn’t just Nanjing where this happened.

In cities where the Evils Faction and Justice Faction coexisted, similar events occurred, with just slight differences.

Kangho, which had been as quiet as a lake, began to stir.

The stones thrown into the Yangtze River shook not just the river but the whole world.

All the beggars in the provinces of Central Plains flew ceaselessly towards the general leaders of the Beggars Union to inform them about the strange events occurring there.

Chapter 786: Either Live Like A Dog Or Die Like A Wolf (1)

“This is insane!”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s face contorted horribly as he read the letter the beggar brought.

“What is it?”

“It-it is a mess, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

“So what is it?”

Unlike Hong Dae-Kwang, whose face went dark, Chung Myung remained calm and nonchalant.

The beggar who delivered the news frowned as he looked at the two very different people.

‘No, why are you here, leaving your place?’
