“I need to pay back. I don’t know if it is enough, but I will have to pay it back.”

Jang Ilso laughed.

“Of course, it remains to be seen if they will like my gift, but in any case, it is meaningful in expressing sincerity.”

Ho Ga-Myung, seeing Jang Ilso’s smile, nodded.

Sometimes, his position as general felt meaningless.

He was unrivaled when it came to using his brains, no matter where he went, and had the confidence to shake anyone with his words. But only one person, Jang Ilso, was inscrutable to him.

However, Ho Ga-Myung didn’t feel anxious about it.

Jang Ilso was Jang Ilso. He wasn’t someone that normal people could judge.

Ho Ga-Myung glanced at Jang Ilso, who had already closed his eyes and was deep in thought. Ho Ga-Myung held his breath so as not to disturb his thoughts.

Jang Ilso closed his eyes and pressed his temples with his fingers.

‘Now is the start.’

It was just one step. There was still a long way to go. And the road ahead would continue to be a difficult and thorny one. Just walking would tear your flesh, and thorns would get stuck in your bones.

However, when he thought about what he would gain by walking there, Jang Ilso was able to walk with a smile, not only on a thorny path but one with fire too.

A hungry person never stopped.

And the day would never come when his hunger vanished.

“No fight?”

“It seems like it.”

Chung Myung suddenly grabbed Im So-Byeong by the collar and shook him.

“What do you mean? If your dirty bastards and vile Justice faction people gather, everyone will not care and just fight! Why aren’t they fighting! Why! I paid down the full plan!”

“Ugh! Leave me alone!”

“No! Don’t you people fight with each other and die too! What is with you!”

Baek Cheon sighed as he heard those words.

“Chung Myung...”

“Uh?”

“You should say things like that where no one can hear.”

“Ah.”

Even Baek Cheon was still unable to handle the fact that a Taoist was saying such a thing.

‘You seem like the apostle.’

Dirty bastards and the vile Justice faction? Did this guy even think before speaking?

“Cough! Cough! That... it seems like the pirates ran without care when they saw the Justice Faction!”

“Can’t they chase? Don’t the Shaolin bastards have footwork? Where are they? This guy and that guy, if they look for them, they will find them all!”

“Footwork is meaningless. They are on ships. So how do they follow?”

“Then get on a ship and chase them!”

“... then won’t the pirates attack the ship? Then what will happen?”

“What happens? What happens then? Good! The bastards are all going to fall into the water; it is a feast!”

“....”

Im So-Byeong, who looked at Chung Myung with blank eyes, helplessly turned his gaze to Baek Cheon.

Baek Cheon, unable to bear it, lowered his head.

‘I am sorry.’

A bandit who lived off the people was questioning the character of a disciple of a Taoist sect. Of course, it was nonsense, but the person who couldn’t refute such nonsense was Chung Myung.

“Anyway... no, leave that aside. Anyway... it seems like they abandoned the pirate base and went to the Yangtze River.”

“They couldn’t continue doing that, though?”

“Probably so... yes, since they have to find help, they will all be flocking to the Black Dragon Pirates.”

“Uh? The Black Dragon Pirates?”

Im So-Byeong nodded.

“They are the head of the 18 River Families of the Yangtze River. However, while the Green Forest is like a mountain temple for bandits, the Black Dragon Pirates serve the same for the pirate troops. The Black Dragon King is not like now, but one who considers himself to be an emperor.”

“Did he tell them to gather together then?”

“No. This is the physiology of bandits and pirates. Even if told to survive on their own, they gather where they can fight each other. I am not sure if this means to ask for help or if they simply cannot die alone.”

“... are they insane?”

“All thieves are like that. Tsk tsk, one never learns.”

Baek Cheon closed his eyes.

‘You are that thief, you!’

“Anyway, so you are saying they are gathering at the Black Dragon Pirates now?”

“I expect it to be so.”

“And Shaolin with Wudang are heading there.”

“Maybe no?”

Chung Myung let go of the hand that was tightly holding Im So-Byeong’s collar and nodded.

“Then let’s go! Sasuk!”

“... Why?”

“Hurry up and tell the Sect Leader to get the guys ready! I am going there!”

“Why now...?”

“Why? Why? If all the pirates and the Justice Faction bastards gather there, a big fight will happen!”

“... Don’t keep calling the Justice Faction bastards.”

He kept speaking like they were the Evil Faction, this bastard!

“What will people say if such a battle is taking place and we are just standing from afar? We laid all the plans in the first place, and if those eat the benefits, I cannot sleep! Don’t delay it and move!”

“So you plan to join in the battle?”

Baek Cheon asked with a serious look. When he heard the word’ battle,’ the passion for winning naturally soared. But at that moment.

“No.”

“... Uh?”

“You want to look at that? Those bastards are killing each other, so why get involved?”

Smile.

Chung Myung poured cold water on Baek Cheon’s burning heart.

Baek Cheon asked helplessly, his shoulders drooping.

“... You said we shouldn’t stay behind earlier?”

“It is fine to go there. People don’t even have to see it in person; all they have to know is that Mount Hua was there.”

“...”

“All you have to do is coax the beggar and tell them to spread the rumor that we fought hard. Or maybe, just prepare some people to spread it. Don’t they believe that?”

“We can spread that around as well. Hehehe.”

“Yes! Hehehe!”

Seeing Im So-Byeong and Chung Myung laughing like that felt like hell.

“Prepare mooncake and alcohol in advance. I actually enjoy drinking alcohol while watching people fight!”

“...”

“Ehehehehe! I hope they all die! Hehehehe!”

Baek Cheon quietly looked up at the clear sky. It was a clear and peaceful day.

‘Oh, Heavens.’

He really needed help now.

Please do something about this bastard...

Please....

Chapter 793: Only He Is Worthy (3)

“....”

Baek Cheon’s face darkened as he looked at the people gathered by the riverside.

“...guys.”

“Yes, sasuk.”

“...did you say the sect leader was going?”

“It seemed like it?”

Yoon Jong added to Jo Gul’s answer.

“Actually, well... it is a bit weird to fall into that kind of situation.”

“First, it seems like Elder Hyun Young was losing his mind...”

“...why?”

“I heard he almost flipped out as soon as he heard that only Wudang would gain fame. It seemed like he was going to grab anyone by the collar at any moment.”

“...”

“Well, it isn’t that I don’t understand how he feels, actually.”

Baek Cheon listened blankly and nodded.

No, well, he could understand to that extent. Actually, even if he stopped, his feelings towards Wudang had been rapidly deteriorating recently. In the past, if he was asked which sect he disliked the most, he would have said Southern Edge without hesitation. Still, now, after taking a deep breath and thinking about it, he would answer Southern Edge.

Uh? Weren’t they the same?

Very different. At least it meant that now they had to take a moment to compare Southern Edge and Wudang.

In the past, there was nothing to compare. Now Wudang was fighting Southern Edge for that spot? To compare them to Southern Edge, they had to be at the level of the Demonic Sect.

“Right, I understand that...”

“Yes, sasuk.”

“Then why are those people doing that? Are they sure they want to go together?”

Baek Cheon’s eyes turned to the bandits and pirates lined up behind Mount Hua’s disciples.

They, too, were all standing in their places with expressions of ‘why are we standing here?’

Right, it must be embarrassing. Baek Cheon was also flustered, so why wouldn’t they be?

Yoon Jong sighed and answered.

“I already tried to stop him once... I don’t get it. He dragged everything away.”

“...why?”

“If you leave the pirates here, they will all run. Even if he dies, he wouldn’t want to see that.”

“...”

Uh... right.

It was certainly true that they shouldn’t let the pirates go free. They were paying for their sins through labor.

If they did something wrong, they had to make amends. If not to kill all the pirates, the role of Mount Hua was also to watch over those held captive to prevent them from being released and causing harm to civilians.

The problem...

“But he is dragging bandits and pirates to a place where the Justice Faction gathers?”

“That would look like a giant mess?”

Baek Cheon couldn’t answer and slowly looked away.

Chung Myung was seen kicking the pirates with excitement.

“No, these pirate bastards couldn’t even stand in a straight line or what! How dare you hold the leg of the sect leader? What? Uncomfortable? Should I show you what really makes you uncomfortable?”

“N-No! I was wrong!”

“If you do something wrong, you need to get hit! Bastards!”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened as he turned away from the pirate’s chin.

Not only did the pirates flinch at it, but even the bandits craned their necks and watched Chung Myung.

“...”

Baek Cheon, who felt a subtle sense of kinship with that wrinkled form, couldn’t bear to look anymore and closed his eyes tightly. When one stood in front of that bastard, everyone became equal regardless of good or bad.

“Nice. I kind of like it now. The preparations are done, sect leader!”

Instead of answering, a sigh escaped from Hyun Jong’s mouth. He moved his lips like he wanted to say something but ended up shaking his head.

‘I understand, sect leader.’

‘We too know.’

It felt like everyone heard what Hyun Jong was trying to say. They even thought about why he didn’t dare to say it out loud.

Wasn’t it the job of a Taoist to chant a mantra into the ears of a cow?

“That...”

Hyun Jong’s shoulders slumped as he looked at the odd combination of Taoists, bandits, and pirates in front of him.

“... let’s leave.”

“Yes!”

Hyun Jong turned around and began to run away without looking back.

Mount Hua, Green Forest, and the Pirates of the Great Whale Pirates—a small number of people ran quickly along the river.

The martial arts sect specialized in running made their home in Mount Hua, and the bandits of Green Forest were elite bandits accustomed to traversing mountainous terrain, so their speed was no joke.

As a result, it was the pirates who were struggling.

“Ughh...”

“I-I am dying...”

However, in the midst of all that, no one dared to be left behind. The reason was simple.

‘Fall behind and you die.’

‘He will really kill that person!’

This was because Chung Myung was following behind them.

‘No, we are running with our backs turned, so why is his speed similar to our running speed?’

‘Is that person a ghost or a human?’

What was certain was that whoever fell behind, human or ghost, would definitely get beaten to death.

“Run quickly, you pirate bastards! Do you think that just because you dug up the riverbed, your sins vanish? How dare you fall behind? Let us reduce the number of rice bowls to give!”

“Ekkk!”

Baek Cheon slowly followed Chung Myung, who was chasing after the running pirates.

“Chung Myung.”

“What?”

“Do we have to hurry there?”

“Oh, Dong-Ryong has grown up so much. Are you worried about the pirates too?”

“It isn’t that, brat! If you have to say it right, it is a matter of showing your face and ending it. But I am saying this because if we arrive early, we might have to fight.”

“Oh?”

At that moment, Chung Myung opened his eyes and looked at Baek Cheon. Baek Cheon, feeling slightly upset, asked bluntly.

“What?”

“No, I thought Sasuk was finally doing what he wanted to do. Until now, I thought that thing on the neck was a high-quality hero’s pride.”

“Are you being for real?”

Baek Cheon closed his eyes and was about to rush in, but just before that, Chung Myung quickly opened his mouth.

“Yes, but.”

“Uh?”

“... I feel a bit uncomfortable.”

“... What?”

“It is just.”

The corners of Chung Myung’s lips slightly twitched as he answered briefly.

‘It was all I had to do up to this point.’

Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance were still weak.

Of course, it was clear that the Heavenly Friends Alliance had established itself as the only force that could deal with the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, but “could deal with” didn’t mean they were equal.

The Heavenly Friends Alliance was still just a weak force, to the point that even a single cough from the Nine Great Sects made their bodies ache.

‘We still need these guys from the Evil Faction.’

The Heavenly Friends Alliance was spreading its influence in all directions. Nevertheless, there was just one reason why the Nine Great Sects had ignored the Heavenly Friends Alliance until now.

Face.

Because those who called themselves noble and right couldn’t openly interfere with the same Justice Faction that was working together.

The reason why Chung Myung was able to beat up the Nine Great Sects until now was because he knew well that face was more important to them.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“Do you know when people lose their face?”

“... Um?”

“When it is time for the food bowl to be snatched.”

“What does all that mean suddenly?”

Instead of answering straight away, Chung Myung sneered.

‘They are almost at their limit.’

Whether it was for the sake of a cause or to save their face, it was necessary to prepare well while the plate was still in good condition. If their blindness to the act of fools continued to grow like this, the day would come when they, too, would eventually throw away their facades.

Hadn’t they realized deep down that the principle of action driving the Nine Great Sects was never mutual consultation?

The moment they thought they were being deprived of the rice they could be eating, they would try to repeat what they did to Mount Hua in the past.

They would attempt to profit by undermining the value of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and by tearing down the sects that belong to it. Just like before, instead of helping Mount Hua when it was under attack, they sought to take advantage of the situation.

‘Even if you hurt us once, I will not let you hurt me twice, you bastards!’

To prevent that from happening, he had to make it so they could not move. If this fight turned into a direct confrontation between the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Nine Great Sects, the outcome was obvious.

‘We cannot win.’

It would take time. Time to make the sects belonging to the Heavenly Friends Alliance stronger and strengthen their bonds even more!

So, it was necessary to divert the attention of the Nine Great Sects immediately. In any case, they were still clinging to their reason and respect.

“Till this point.”

“... What are you saying? Speak clearly!”

Chung Myung frowned.

“But things seem to be going strangely.”

“Uh?”

“The fact that they were gathering at the Black Dragon Pirates means that the Black Dragon Pirates didn’t abandon the Yangtze River and retreat.”

“... Right. Because we wouldn’t gather in a place where they are not around.”

“That makes no sense.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung rolled his eyes.

“The pirates are so stubborn that they are holding on even though Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung are attacking at once.”

“Qingcheng, too.”

“Except for the clams.”

Baek Cheon was stunned. If the sect leader of Qingcheng heard this now, he would have walked away.

Was there anyone in the world, other than this guy, who called a sect belonging to the Nine Great Sects a clam?

“Looking at the pirates’ behavior, it’s wise to run away first. These people are different from the Nine Great Sects. They don’t respect anything. They don’t care about their reputation or fame.”

“...no matter what, they are a clan. Why should they run while their men are still fighting?”

“The leaders of the Evil Faction don’t operate like that. First, they have to survive to have power and subordinates. In the Justice Faction, the basics come first, but among the Evil Faction, the self comes first.”

“...”

“But the Black Dragon pirates are holding on.”

Chung Myung scratched his chin and was lost in thought.

‘There is a part of me that believes in it.’

He had to see this with his own eyes. If what he thought was happening, he might have to change his entire plan.

If everything went according to his plan, they would have to retreat from the Yangtze River. But not stepping on that path meant one of two things.

One was that they lost.

The other...

‘Someone saw what I couldn’t see.’

A smile came across Chung Myung’s lips as he was lost in thought.

‘So fun.’

Who could it be?

He didn’t know. No, he didn’t want to know.

But Chung Myung somehow seemed to know who. Who was the one responsible for making him feel so uncomfortable?

One face kept appearing in Chung Myung’s mind.

Two black, twinkling eyes were visible above blood-red lips.

‘Jang Ilso.’

In two lifetimes, he had met countless people. In terms of experience, no one in this Kangho could even come close to Chung Myung.

But he swore that it was the first time Chung Myung had faced such strange eyes.

‘Different.’

It was different from the madness in the eyes of the Demonic Sect.

It was different from the endless emptiness that clouded the eyes of the Heavenly Demon.

That...

“Chung Myung! Ahead!”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung, who was deep in thought, suddenly came to his senses and raised his head.

He saw a group of warriors approaching him.

‘Enemy?’

No, not them.

Chung Myung frowned and looked at them. Both eyes shone meaningfully.

A neatly dressed warrior with a long sword in hand. The vivid Taiji symbol is engraved on the chest.

“Ha...”

Chung Myung laughed, shrugged his shoulders, and mumbled.

“This... precious person came to meet us. I am so grateful.”

Wudang.

Perhaps the people he wanted to encounter were approaching at high speed. The cold light that flowed from the eyes of the Wudang Sect leader, Venerable Heo Do, who led them from the front, shot towards Mount Hua.

Chapter 794: Only He Is Worthy (4)

“Sect leader!”

Venerable Heo Do turned his head when he heard a voice calling him. Soon, his eyebrows furrowed slightly.

People wearing black warrior robes ran towards them, followed by groups of people wearing green and blue clothes.

It was truly a strange combination, but Venerable Heo Do was able to immediately figure out their identity.

“Mount Hua...”

A slightly suppressed voice came from Venerable Heo Do. The only people who made such a fuss among the current sects were Mount Hua.

He ended up encountering the most annoying of them on the way to the Black Dragon Pirates.

“... what do we do?”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone at Heo Sanja’s question.

“There is no reason to avoid them. We did nothing wrong.”

Soon, an odd smile formed on his lips.

If he thought about it, he hadn’t seen Mount Hua in so long since the martial arts tournament. He had only heard stories of them.

‘I guess I need to check them out with my own eyes.’

Having made his decision, Venerable Heo Do spoke briefly.

“Heo San.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“I need to meet the Mount Hua sect.”

“.... Sect leader. There is no need to do that...”

Heo Sanja was flustered and started to dissuade Venerable Heo Do.

It wasn’t because encountering Mount Hua was uncomfortable.

Because they were Justice Factions, things weren’t strictly divided into higher and lower sects like other sects. Still, implicitly, the levels were naturally divided.

The reputation and skills of Wudang were incomparably higher than those of Mount Hua. If they followed the logic, they shouldn’t approach Mount Hua, but Mount Hua should come to them. If Venerable Heo Do had spoken first, he might have put more weight on Mount Hua’s shoulders for no reason.

Venerable Heo Do, who must have guessed Heo Sanja’s thoughts, spoke with a disapproving look.

“Are you still obsessed with that?”

“... Sect leader, still....”

“It has become something of a useless, vain act. What matters is what you get.”

When it was said like this, Heo Sanja finally, reluctantly, nodded.

Now that Venerable Heo Do had decided, further discussion would be a waste.

“Let’s go.”

“Yes.”

When Venerable Heo Do changed direction, the disciples of the Wudang Sect who followed him headed towards Mount Hua.

Meanwhile, Hyun Jong, observing the scene, groaned inwardly.

‘Umm.’

The qi emitted by the best of Wudang took his breath away.

‘Wudang...’

These were people who had been through actual combat. Although he wasn’t actively releasing qi, the heavy atmosphere made it natural to think so.

“They are coming this way, Sect Leader.”

“I know.”

Hyun Jong gulped and watched them.

In the past, he might not have had the courage to face the Wudang Sect leader. But just as the current Mount Hua was different from the past, his position wasn’t the same as before.

It was time to show a proud demeanor as the Sect Leader of Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

‘But...’

Hyun Jong’s back was soaked with cold sweat.

His position had changed, but it was clear that the way they treated Hyun Jong must have changed just as much if not more.

The look from Venerable Heo Do, who was in the lead, weighed heavily on his shoulders.

It was then.

“They sneak up on me like that to say they have something to pick with us?”

Hyun Jong glanced back at the voice that broke the tension.

Before he knew it, Chung Myung had caught up behind him and was looking at Wudang with both hands on the back of his head.

“... Chung Myung.”

“Looks like their asses are on fire. In the past, even if they had seen us, they would have ignored us.”

“...”

“Sect Leader.”

“Uh?”

“Do not be too harsh. These days, I am feeling burned out, so they need to be accepted with a big heart. But if you know them, they are our descendants, too, right?”

“...”

Hyun Jong listened to those words in a daze and burst out laughing.

The only person in the world who could speak of Wudang in such a way was Chung Myung alone. Even the sect leader of Wudang wouldn’t look down on Wudang.

“Phew.”

Well, thanks to that, it felt like the pressure on his body, which was stopping his movement, had disappeared like a lie. Now, he was able to look at Venerable Heo Do with a comfortable expression.

As the distance between the two sects grew closer, their steps slowed down. Venerable Heo Do approached Hyun Jong at a leisurely pace.

Hyun Jong quietly put both hands together and held them out.

“I greet the Wudang sect leader.”

“I, Heo Do of Wudang, greet Mount Hua’s sect leader.”

Heo Do also bowed. He bowed his head deeply, then raised it and spoke with a kind expression.

“It seems like this is the first time we have met each other since the Worldly Martial Arts Tournament, sect leader.”

“True. I am happy to have met you again.”

Greetings were exchanged as if performing a ritual, and Venerable Heo Do smiled.

“At that time, I had no idea that Mount Hua would become this famous. I congratulate you on that.”

“That is a lot of praise. No matter how famous Mount Hua is, how can it be compared to Wudang, which is the North Star of the world? We just got lucky.”

“Hahahah. Isn’t excessive humility bad? The sect leader has now become an Alliance leader. If you say that even that is luck, the other members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance will be heartbroken.”

“Thanks to everyone looking at me favorably. If we had decided to become a leader based on skills and fame, how could I have achieved that position? My shoulders are weighed down by a heavy burden.”

Jo Gul whispered to Yoon Jong, who was listening to the conversation.

“Sahyung, sahyung. They both speak so softly and yet listening to them gives me a scary feeling.”

“Gul.”

“Yes?”

“...shut your mouth. If you get into trouble here, your bones will break.”

“...Yes.”

Jo Gul quietly covered his mouth with both hands.

At that time, Venerable Heo Do quietly looked at the people standing behind Hyun Jong.

“If you don’t mind, may I ask who the people standing behind them are?”

“Those are the people of Green Forest 72 Bandits.”

“... I see.”

Venerable Heo Do nodded his head silently. That was all the reaction he gave.

Hyun Jong, who had expected to be criticized for bringing the Evils Faction along, looked at him with a slightly odd expression.

He never thought Venerable Heo Do would be this silent.

“What is it?”

“Ah... I thought I had to come up with an excuse.”

Venerable Heo Do smiled at Hyun Jong’s words.

“Isn’t it true that the sect leader took in even the bandits of Green Forest through high virtue? I am just jealous. Even if I pursue Tao all my life, I don’t think I can follow it that strictly.”

“... such excessive praise.”

Hyun Jong lowered his head in embarrassment.

Venerable Heo Do smiled happily and looked around at Mount Hua’s disciples.

‘So sharp.’

The qi which flowed implicitly was no less than that of Wudang. Of course, it was less serious than Wudang, and at first glance, felt light. Even with empty words, it could not be said to be serious Taoist qi...

‘Instead, it is clear and free.’

A new feeling suddenly occurred to him.

‘Since when did something heavy and serious come to represent Taoism?’

In the first place, the Tao should have been untethered.

Venerable Heo Do, who was lost in thought for a moment, then nodded heavily and looked at Hyun Jong.

“In the past, in the martial arts tournament...”

When the word “martial arts tournament” came up, the face of Hyun Jong hardened.

The duels that took place in Wuhan.

It might be a happy memory for Mount Hua, but for Venerable Heo Do, it was a terrible memory. Venerable Heo Do was bringing up the story in front of Mount Hua.

“We were very upset because our disciples had been humiliated.”

“... We just got lucky.”

Hyun Jong responded with an uncomfortable feeling, but surprisingly, Venerable Heo Do was cool about it.

“I also had that thought deep down. But after seeing Mount Hua disciples today, it seemed that it wasn’t just luck.”

Venerable Heo Do once again addressed Hyun Jong.

“I have learned a lot, Sect Leader.”

“Don’t say that. I haven’t done anything.”

“If it wasn’t for the Sect Leader’s work, how could Mount Hua have reached what it is today?”

The moment Hyun Jong was about to utter words of humility once again, Venerable Heo Do’s gaze fell squarely on someone else.

“Isn’t that right, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

“Our Sect Leader is quite amazing.”

“Hahaha. That is what I said.”

“As expected, he is at the level of Wudang’s Sect Leader, so you know exactly! This is why people need to be in good status.”

“Hahahaha!”

“Ahahaha!”

The two looked at each other and smiled as if they got along well.

But, unlike their bright expressions on the outside, their eyes were keenly observing and understanding each other.

“You took down Heo Gong?”

“Yes, with skill.”

“...”

Venerable Heo Do was speechless and looked back at Hyun Jong for a moment. But Hyun Jong had turned his gaze to the distant sky.

It was an expression that said, ‘I cannot do anything about him, handle him yourself.’

“Skill... right. It was skill. Because Heo Gong isn’t someone who can be defeated by luck.”

“Yes. It was amazing.”

“Aren’t you more amazing for defeating someone like that?”

“Hehe. I didn’t mean it that way, but since you said it so clearly, it is hard for me to deny it. Ahem.”

“...”

Venerable Heo Do looked around Mount Hua once again, but no one saw him. This was because their disciple, who would be embarrassing to display anywhere, was now pushing his stomach out in front of Wudang’s Sect Leader.

‘I want to hide.’

‘I feel like I am going to die of embarrassment.’

“Haha.”

Venerable Heo Do laughed and nodded.

“Nice. There is no hesitation in calling amazing as amazing. Lao Tzu also said that unnecessary courtesy eats away at human results.”

Hearing these refreshing words, Chung Myung looked at Venerable Heo Do with a bright smile in his eyes.

“You better be careful, though. Our kids are still training, forgetting about the past, in order to defeat you.”

“A challenge is always welcome.”

“Challenge...”

Venerable Heo Do, who thought for a moment, smiled.

“Having said that next time our kids challenge you, don’t back down because it’s annoying.”

“Uh... I cannot promise you that.”

“Haha. Then there is nothing we can do.”

Venerable Heo Do finished the light talk and spoke to Hyun Jong.

“I have many stories I want to share, but it’s a shame I cannot chat more because I have to hurry on my way.”

“Wouldn’t there be another chance?”

“Yes. I will visit you again and ask for your guidance.”

Venerable Heo Do politely handed a bow.

“Then.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

When Hyun Jong did the same, Venerable Heo Do turned around without hesitation.

“Let’s go.”

“Yes!”

The Wudang Sect left first, leaving Mount Hua standing still. It was only after they were a little further away that a deep sigh escaped Hyun Jong’s mouth.

“... ah.”

It wasn’t surprising how much Wudang hated Mount Hua.

However, Venerable Heo Do didn’t lose his politeness and respect even for a moment. Actually, he even praised Mount Hua.

Hyun Jong thought about whether he could have done that.

‘That is what sect leaders of famous sects are like.’

A person whose posture was humble, but from that humble posture, one could feel the power to overwhelm the opponent. If Venerable Heo Do had openly exposed his teeth, they might not have felt this much pressure.

And the Wudang Sect supporting him also didn’t reveal any emotions they might have accumulated during the previous meetings.

‘There is still a long way to go.’

Neither he nor Mount Hua was good enough to compare with Wudang.

“Hmm.”

Meanwhile, Chung Myung, who was watching Wudang, had folded his arms and stood behind Hyun Jong, his lips curled into a smile.

‘Still doing well, eh?’

There were no instances of the Wudang members, including the Venerable Heo Do, being rude.

But Chung Myung was annoyed by Heo Do’s attitude because being able to maintain himself properly meant that they still had time to spare.

“Let us see how long your leisurely attitude will last.”

Chung Myung’s eyes were sharp as they looked at the Wudang sect.

Chapter 795: Only He Is Worthy (5)

“Sahyung.”

“Uh?”

While running towards the Black Dragon pirates, Jo Gul spoke slowly.

“That... Do you know the name of the Wudang sect leader?”

“Venerable Heo Do?”

“Yes, him.”

“What about him?”

“Don’t you think he’s a bit different from what we imagined?”

“Uh?”

As Yoon Jong looked at him with a confused expression, Jo Gul expressed his concern.

“I thought he would be a very sharp swordsman because he’s the sect leader of Wudang. But when I saw him, he seemed like a kind person.”

Of course, this wasn’t the first time they had seen the sect leader of Wudang.

In the past, they had seen Venerable Heo Do sitting on the platform at the tournament, but that was from afar.

This was the first time they had confirmed what kind of person Venerable Heo Do was.

“He’s a good person...”

Yoon Jong repeated those words for a moment and then looked at Jo Gul with pitying eyes.

“Why are you looking at me like that?”

“Gul.”

“Uh?”

“It’s a good thing you came to Mount Hua.”

“Hehe. What are you saying? You’re embarrassing me.”

“If you had followed in the footsteps of your father and become a merchant, you would have raised the entire family.”

“...”

Yoon Jong clicked his tongue and shook his head.

Tang Soso, who was listening, supported his words.

“Right, Jo Gul sahyung. The sect leaders of the great sects shouldn’t be judged by what they show. We don’t know what’s inside.”

Jo Gul tilted his head as if he didn’t understand at all.

“Are you saying the Tang family lord is like that, too?”

“It’s because my father is unusually friendly to Mount Hua, but that’s not the case when dealing with other sects. People who have risen to such a level are those who hide their true feelings as if it were second nature.”

“Umm.”

“True.”

Baek Cheon nodded in agreement.

“We ended up exchanging pleasantries, but there’s no way the purpose of coming all this way and talking was just about greetings. They probably figured out a lot of things we cannot assume.”

“... for that short period?”

“So, he must be the Wudang sect leader.”

Baek Cheon’s eyes shone.

“If there is something to learn, learn it; if there is something to imitate, imitate it. But no matter the situation, never neglect to be alert.”

“Yes, sasuk!”

“I will keep that in mind.”

Meanwhile, Chung Myung, who was watching this from behind, chuckled.

‘They’ve grown well.’

In the past, people who would have made a big fuss just because they met the Wudang sect leader are now wary and worried in their own way.

“First, focus on the task at hand. Our opponent now is the Black Dragon Pirates, not Wudang.”

“Yes, sasuk!”

The sight of him running ahead, full of motivation, was truly heroic. Chung Myung grinned.

“You are very handsome.”

Well, whatever. No matter how young you were, you had to do this much.

Tat.

Venerable Heo Do, who was in the lead, quickly ran ahead.

The gentle expression on his face that he had previously shown in front of Hyun Jong and the disciples of the Mount Hua sect had long since disappeared. All that remained was cold, hard determination.

“How was it?”

Heo Sanja approached cautiously and asked.

Then Venerable Heo Do opened his mouth without any change in his expression.

“You mean Mount Hua?”

“Yes.”

He had heard many things about Mount Hua.

But that was mostly just an evaluation of Mount Hua’s actions. What Heo Sanja wanted to hear was Venerable Heo Do’s evaluation of Mount Hua as he had seen it with his own eyes.

“It is clear that we must be vigilant. But...”

Venerable Heo Do trailed off slightly. He closed his mouth as if he was a little worried, but only after a while did he slowly open it again.

“Mount Hua is definitely a great sect. When I saw them in person, I could feel their greatness.”

It was something close to a compliment. Heo Sanja pondered for a moment, wondering if such a favorable review had ever come from Venerable Heo Do’s mouth.

“But that makes it more painful—the time they lost.”

“...”

“I was fortunate to see Mount Hua with my own eyes.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes darkened.

The Mount Hua they actually saw was more threatening than he had thought and even more amazing. There was definitely something in Mount Hua that couldn’t be found in Wudang.

But...

‘That clarity is their weakness.’

It was a place that could not yet be called their enemy, but it was fortunate to have had the chance to closely understand a place that could become their enemy. That alone made this expedition worthwhile.

‘Once the distance is widened, it cannot be narrowed so easily.’

Because they had underestimated it for so long, something like this happened. However, if they recognized Mount Hua as Mount Hua and stayed alert, they were confident that they could maintain the gap with them.

This time, it felt like it.

In the past, the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families would have just watched as Mount Hua swept away all the public sentiment in the Yangtze River and took all the profits they could. But this time, as soon as they heard that Mount Hua would appear on the Yangtze River and fight against the pirates, they took notice and flocked.

That was what it meant to be an object of caution.

‘Sect leader, anyone who climbs up must bear that much weight.’

It wasn’t that difficult to turn a hundredth place into a tenth place. But, to become the first place, several times more effort was needed to reach that position.

Mount Hua, which has already earned the wariness of other sects, will no longer be able to make one-sided gains like in the past.

“But...”

“Uh?”

“No, it is nothing.”

Venerable Heo Do’s face hardened strangely. A feeling akin to tension arose.

’If the world remained as it is now, this distance would not narrow. But...’

What if the world wasn’t as peaceful as it is now? Would Wudang be able to maintain the gap with Mount Hua even then?

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone coldly.

‘I will be busy when I return to Wudang.’

To achieve that, the first task was to deal with the Black Dragon Pirates.

Venerable Heo Do channeled Qi into his leg and kicked the ground hard.

“Oh...”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had been running continuously, finally arrived at the site of the Black Dragon Pirates.

Everyone’s eyes widened at the scene unfolding before them.

“S-sect leader.”

“Hmm.”

How should this scene be described?

Disciplined?

No, no. It wasn’t appropriate to describe the scene with words that had such a positive connotation.

Several groups of warriors were crowded together on the riverside.

First, the most noticeable were the Shaolin monks in yellow robes. Then, there were the disciples of Wudang who had arrived before them.

Next to them were people in sky-blue robes.

Shaolin, Wudang, Namgung, and Qingcheng.

The four sects that ruled the world were gathered on the riverside. It was truly a breathtaking sight.

‘Has there ever been a gathering of people like this in one place since the last Demonic Sect war?’

It finally felt real. The fact that times were changing.

Even though the people gathered here weren’t showing hostility to Mount Hua, they would feel a body-crushing pressure. If that was the case for them, too, how overwhelming must it feel for the pirates they had to deal with?

If one was in Kangho, they would hear a lot about it, so the warriors of the sects boldly drew their swords and left their base.

“Sect leader.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“Right, let’s go.”

The hands hidden inside the long sleeves were shaking, but now was the time to move forward resolutely because he was Mount Hua’s sect leader.

Mount Hua’s disciples began to take the steps they had once halted.

They, too, understood what it meant to join that place, and so their steps were filled with spirit.

Beop Gye, who saw Mount Hua approaching, slowly walked to greet them.

“Amitabha.”

“I greet Mount Hua’s sect leader.”

“Elder Beop Gye. I feel glad.”

Beop Gye nodded.

It wasn’t that he was particularly kind to Mount Hua. Still, personal circumstances weren’t important when meeting on behalf of a sect. What was important was the fact that the sect leader of Mount Hua spoke friendly words to Shaolin.

“Thank you for coming here. Everyone in the world will be amazed by the majesty of Mount Hua.”

Hyun Jong smiled at Beop Gye’s words.

But the moment Chung Myung heard those words, his eyes suddenly turned upside down.

“No, that bald... eupp!”

“Hahahaha. Right. Chung Myung! You say that all sentient beings will be thankful to Mount Hua! Our Chung Myung is so special! So interested in Buddhism! I know everything about sentient beings! Hahahaha!”

Baek Cheon laughed and desperately covered Chung Myung’s mouth. Even Hae Yeon was holding Chung Myung’s neck and squeezing it quietly with a pale face.

“Eup! Eup! T-that bald...!”

“Y-yes. Seeing it before you knew it! That is a duck! Is this your first time seeing a duckling? I find it fascinating, too!”

In that gap, the Five Swords quickly ran over and tightly wrapped Chung Myung’s mouth with a cloth, tying up his entire body.

“Eupp! Eupp!”

Chung Myung quickly turned into a pupa-like being, struggling to move, but no one felt sorry for him.

“Phew. Put it all the way back there. No, no. It would be better to take this chance and bury him.”

“Is it okay?”

“... No, don’t kill.”

Baek Cheon sighed and looked at Chung Myung being dragged away.

It was Mount Hua who began the subjugation, so Mount Hua must be in a position to welcome them. However, Beop Gye cleverly and naturally expressed gratitude to Mount Hua.

It was as if they were the protagonist, and Mount Hua was merely helping Shaolin’s intentions.

‘A person called a monk!’

He couldn’t say that the feelings for the sect were wrong, but wasn’t that too cowardly and petty?

Hae Yeon’s face turned red as if he understood their feelings.

At that time, Beop Gye glanced at Hae Yeon and said to Hyun Jong.

“Sect leader.”

“Yes, elder.”

“Hae Yeon.”

Hae Yeon politely greeted the disciples of Mount Hua. The Mount Hua disciples then came together and showed respect to Hae Yeon.

Although he wasn’t a disciple of Mount Hua, it was the highest courtesy that could be shown to someone who was no different from a Mount Hua disciple.

Hyun Jong looked at Hae Yeon with slightly sad eyes.

Even though he wasn’t a disciple of Mount Hua, it felt like losing a disciple. His heart felt heavy as if he were going through a breakup.

After confirming that Hae Yeon had crossed over, Beop Gye suddenly looked to the side. He said to Hyun Jong,

“Rather than that, I think we should greet each other.”

From the direction Beop Gye was looking, the head of the Namgung family and the Sect Leader of the Qingcheng sect were walking straight to them.

Chapter 796: If You Are Handsome And Pathetic, You Are One Real Loser (1)

“I am Namgung Hwang.”

“I am Byeok Hyun.”

Hyun Jong bowed to the two people before him.

“I am Hyun Jong of Mount Hua. I am grateful to see you again.“

They were all familiar with the past Martial Arts Tournament. Still, the attitude of the two had noticeably changed towards Hyun Jong.

First was Byeok Hyun.

He, the sect leader of the Qingcheng sect, smiled brightly as he looked at Hyun Jong.

“Mount Hua’s fame often reaches Sichuan. I had a feeling Mount Hua would become renowned during the tournament, but I cannot help but admire the performance that surpassed my expectations. I congratulate you, sect leader.”

“That is such an exaggeration. I find it hard to handle.”

“So much praise. No one would say that, though. I couldn’t be more reassured with Mount Hua coming in a situation where we had to deal with the Evil faction.”

Hyun Jong’s face turned red.

Nevertheless, for some reason, he felt a strain on his shoulders because he could sense that the sect leader of Qingcheng was being pretentious and not sincere.

But.

“What is Mount Hua doing here?”

It seemed like Namgung Hwang didn’t think so.

He stared at Hyun Jong with cold eyes.

“The strength gathered here is already sufficient to handle the pirates. Increasing the number of people will only lead to confusion. Since you came late, just provide support from the back instead of clumsily interfering and causing new trouble.”

“...”

“There is no room for the Heavenly Friends Alliance here.”

Byeok Hyun frowned.

“Lord Namgung, aren’t you being too harsh?”

“What do you mean by that? I am right. Even if Mount Hua started it, didn’t Mount Hua stay on the island and play around while we fought the other pirates?”

Hyun Jong’s eyes trembled.

‘Play around...’

Could that be called playing around?

To those who didn’t know the situation, it might look as such. Life was inherently a comedy when viewed from afar and a tragedy when viewed up close.

Namgung Hwang glared at Hyun Jong with a disapproving look.

Actually, it might be the Wudang who felt the worst about Mount Hua being there. Still, the Namgung family had their own grievances.

The Wudang lost its reputation after the fight with Mount Hua. Although it was tough to get close to them because of similar tendencies, the actual loss they suffered wasn’t great.

However, the Namgung family lost too much because of Mount Hua.

First, the Tang family broke away from the Five Great Families.

Although it wasn’t officially stated, joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance meant breaking away from them. For the Namgung family, who led that alliance, the departure of the Tang family was like cutting off an arm.

Second, Beijing’s commerce had been taken over.

Among the Five Great Families, the largest financial sources were the Namgung and Peng families. Namgung established a commercial sphere centered on Anhui, but the Hubei Peng family dominated the commercial sphere in Hubei.

However, thanks to the damned Eunha merchants’ special deliveries, the balance of business was in danger of being broken as high-ranking officials sought Eunha Special Delivery. Naturally, the burden on the Namgung family increased.

Money and power.

In the midst of attacking both, no matter how good of a person Namgung Hwang was, he wouldn’t view Mount Hua favorably.

And above all...

‘Damn it. Do-wei was put to shame because of the damn Mount Hua.’

Other sects also suffered great humiliation from Mount Hua.

But the weight of the disgrace of a disciple of a sect and the disgrace of the young lord, who would be the next head of the family, were too different to be compared.

Because of that situation, Namgung Hwang couldn’t even bear to see Mount Hua, at least not where he was present.

“What do you think, elder? Are you thinking of moving with Mount Hua?”

“Hmm.”

Beop Gye was silent and didn’t answer right away. This wasn’t something he could hastily respond to. No matter how he answered, it was clear that there would be problems.

Usually, matters like this were discussed quietly behind the scenes, but thanks to Namgung Hwang’s direct approach, he was even more at a loss for what to say. There were too many eyes watching.

“What do you think, sect leader?”

Venerable Heo Do, who was slowly walking in their direction, smiled when asked the question.

“If they thought about it, wasn’t this attack on the Yangtze River possible because there was Mount Hua?”

“...Hmm.”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes frowned slightly. Venerable Heo Do continued,

“No one can deny that Mount Hua played a pivotal role.”

“Sect leader....”

“So.”

Venerable Heo Do interrupted Namgung Hwang.

“Give us the credit in this plan, sect leader. We came to the Yangtze to seek some kind of agreement, but if Mount Hua takes all the credit, we won’t stand a chance.”

“...”

A flustered look flashed across Hyun Jong’s face as he looked at Venerable Heo Do. He had already guessed what that meant, but he didn’t expect him to say it so directly.

“Please.”

When Venerable Heo Do slightly lowered his head, Hyun Jong quickly waved his hand.

“D-don’t do so. This isn’t a big deal for me to show disrespect. I will do as everyone expects.”

“Thank you.”

Venerable Heo Do smiled and continued.

“It isn’t about the merit. As you can see, the place is as such.”

“Hmm.”

What Venerable Heo Do pointed to was across the river. Hyun Jong’s face grew complex.

‘The place is....’

The river wasn’t wide. Considering the width of the Yangtze River they had seen so far, it was rather narrow. If they put their minds to it, he didn’t think it would be much trouble.

But the problem is next.

“In there....”

Across the river was a high, steep cliff. In the middle of it, there was a narrow valley that seemed to have been formed by the heavens being split with an axe.

“You are saying the Black Dragon base is in there?”

“Yes. It is barely wide enough for a large ship to pass.”

“...”

Beop Gye continued to explain.

“I tried to enter by turning around, but it wasn’t so easy. First of all, the cliff was too high to jump off. We would have to go down by rope, and it’s a big deal to get a rope that long... considering the skills of those hidden inside, we will have to take damage when climbing down.”

“... Um, that is right.”

A while back, when Mount Hua attacked the great Whale Pirates, the Green Forest bandits descended down the cliffs.

However, this was possible because Mount Hua managed to block the archers and drew attention to those coming down to prevent the pirates from killing them.

If it was a reasonable height, they could take advantage of being on top, but this height would only endanger those who enter.

“But the only way to attack from above is to go down the cliff, right? Falling rocks or something....”

“You expect us to pour boiling water onto them?”

Even before Hyung Jong finished speaking, Namgung Hwang spoke in a sarcastic tone.

“Mount Hua has a reputation for causing apt damage, but I guess it was just an exaggeration.”

He scoffed and said,

“Are you saying you expect people to be down there and make such blind attacks? In the Black Dragon Pirates, there could be captive civilians or laborers. And throw rocks at them?”

“...”

“Those don’t even sound like apt words.”

Hyun Jong sighed. A new frustrated one escaped his lips.

It was extreme, but he wasn’t wrong. Those who came to the pirates to negotiate could not attack blindly without considering the damage to the civilians.

“I think it would be right for you, the Namgung family head, to take a bit of a breather.”

“Ahem.”

Namgung Hwang cleared his throat loudly at Beop Gye’s cautionary words. Beop Gye summarized the situation.

“So, it looks like we will have to enter through the path ahead. The civilians are harmed, but the elite pirates will not be affected by attacks from above.”

“Then...”

“Too many people will only narrow the path. It is difficult to even distinguish between the sects that will attack and those that won’t, so I think Mount Hua, which arrived last, will have to step back.”

“... I understand.”

Now that even Beop Gye had said so, Hyun Jong had no other choice.

Above all, the four sects linked by the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were moving. One couldn’t help but feel uncomfortable with Hyun Jong, a member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Thank you for understanding.”

“It was nothing...”

Although the conversation took place a little distance away, it was clearly audible to Baek Cheon. His face twitched in anger.

‘Damn them.’

Some people expressed hostility coldly, others politely and more gently.

Everyone had their own methods, but everyone rejected Mount Hua. Mount Hua was the first to fight against the pirates and risked war with them.

‘Is this their way then?’

Just as Baek Cheon bit his lip, holding back his anger.

Step.

‘Um?’

A person could be seen walking forward.

With just one step, Baek Cheon could tell that the approach wasn’t being done out of kindness.

Baek Cheon, who quietly examined the face, was shocked.

‘Namgung Do-wei?’

In the past, Namgung Do-wei was a warrior defeated by Chung Myung in the tournament. As he approached Mount Hua, his face was cold and expressionless, like he wore armor on his face.

Step.

Namgung Do-wei stood right in front of Baek Cheon and quietly opened his mouth.

“Where is Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

“... Uh?”

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

Baek Cheon turned his head a bit instead of answering.

At the same time, everyone’s eyes focused on Baek Cheon.

“Eup! Epepp!”

“...”

“...”

Namgung Do-wei’s eyes trembled.

‘I am sorry.’

Baek Cheon sincerely apologized to Namgung Do-wei from the deepest part of his heart. Considering how far Namgung Do-wei had traveled, he shouldn’t have witnessed that. The minimum courtesy to people...

“R-release him!”

“Is that alright, though?”

“Just release him!”

“Yes!”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong untied the cloth around Chung Myung’s mouth and loosened the iron rope around his body.

“Puaaah! Yah! Shouldn’t you tie it loosely enough for me to breathe? I thought I was going to die!”

“That isn’t important. You have a guest, Chung Myung.”

“Uh? Guest?”

Only then did Chung Myung turn his head and look at Namgung Do-wei.

“Ah....”

Namgung Do-wei’s face went stiff, and he opened his mouth with a heavy voice.

“It’s been so long...”

“... Who?”

Namgung Do-wei was speechless at that and turned his head to the distant sky.

And Baek Cheon, unable to see that, turned his head away.

‘I am really sorry.’

Maybe tying him would have been better.

At that time, clueless Jo Gul whispered to Chung Myung.

“It is Namgung Do-wei! Namgung Do-wei! That Namgung Do-wei! The single blade!”

“Ah? Namgung Do-wei?”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung looked and said ‘ah’ and looked at Namgung Do-wei for a moment.
